Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-25 [session membership records linked or ingested]: ("Yes, record both"; drop census question 2) a v1 `session_in_a_dataset` keeps what its dropped fields mean: the session `part_of` dataset relation's `method.term` says whether the session was INGESTED into the dataset or LINKED into it (`is_linked`; NDI `ndi.dataset` add_ingested_session / add_linked_session), and for a linked session `method.parameters` hold the opener class (`session_creator`) and its arguments, among them the session's path (`session_creator_input2`): where that session's data is stored. Inputs that only restate the session's reference or id are dropped as copies. Terms: an existing ontology term first, a new EMPTY term only if none fits (T19). Replaces the drop in `session_in_a_dataset.m` and the `linked_session` document of did_v2_linked_session_plan.md ([linked_session withdrawn], there). Built: DID-matlab f6486be (#250: `jMembershipBodies.m` `membershipMethod`, checked 2026-10-29); the cases it left open are [session opener inputs] and [linked session reference follows the ingested rule]

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-25 [dataset form terms]: ("b", drop census question 3) a v1 `metadata_editor`'s `ExperimentalApproach`, `TechniquesEmployed` and `DataType` are kept as term assertions about the dataset entity: experimental approach by the existing binding (EMPTY:0000356, the 40 openMINDS `experimentalApproach` terms, [term registry closed value sets]); techniques and data type by two new dataset bindings valued by openMINDS terms (technique; semantic data type). A form value that is not in the list carries the form whole as v1 rather than being dropped. Replaces their drop as "per-subject projections" in `metadata_editor.m`. Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [presentation app is software]: ("a", drop census question 4) a v1 `stimulus_presentation`'s `app` block becomes a `software` entity referenced by the presentation manipulation's `software_id`, with the run's OS and interpreter in its execution environment, deduplicated like all other software: rule R1 (TEAM-SIGN-OFF [software]), as DID's `jSoftwareFromApp` places `app` everywhere else. NDI's `stimulusPresentationToItem` dropped it (1,428 presentations: 20211116 11, Dab 1,242, Soph 175, drop census). Built: NDI-matlab 9b99061 (#1072)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [control selection on the presentation method]: ("b", drop census question 5) a v1 `control_stimulus_ids`'s `control_stimulus_id_method` (how controls were chosen: `method`, e.g. "pseudorandom" or Dab's "psuedorandom", verbatim; `controlid` with `controlid_value`, e.g. `isblank` = 1) becomes method parameters on the presentation's `manipulation`, and its `app` becomes a `software` entity (R1, as [presentation app is software]). A covariate entry has no method slot (`data_body` `covariates`), so the rule goes on the manipulation. Adds to [control as covariate] (2026-10-22), which did not name these two. Not built as decided: replaced the same day by [control pairing is a calculation].

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [lossless means round trip]: ("as long as the information is 100% recoverable, that is lossless. Consider, can V2 be migrated back to V1. If yes, it's lossless.") the test of [generic stage is lossless] is a round trip: a v1 value may be left unwritten only when it is 100% recoverable from what V2 keeps, so that the V2 documents could be migrated back to the v1 ones. A value that only restates another kept value passes; a value nothing else states does not.

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [hartley leftovers checked]: ("7a", drop census question 7) the `hartley_calc` migrator drops `stimulus_properties` only when its VALUES (not only its keys) equal the presentation's generator settings, and `dimension_labels` only when empty, as TEAM-SIGN-OFF [receptive field fold] (2026-08-17) says ("dropped only when equal"); otherwise the document is carried whole as v1. Today 210/210 are equal and empty (drop census). Built: DID-matlab f6486be (#250)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [restated values checked]: ("8a", drop census question 8) values that only restate a kept value stay unwritten ([lossless means round trip]): `ngrid.data_size` (from the datum type), `neuron_extracellular` `number_of_samples_per_channel` / `number_of_channels` (the waveform's axis lengths), a calculator block's inner `depends_on` copy (the top-level edges), `daqreader_ndr.ndi_daqreader_ndr_class` (the reader class). Where the code does not yet check the equality (the inner `depends_on`, the NDR class) a check is added; when a check fails (these two, and the neuron counts, which quarantine today) the document is carried whole as v1. Built: DID-matlab f6486be (#250)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [image file details placed]: ("b"; "yes, record it that way", drop census question 9) a v1 `image` / `imageStack` that becomes a data body keeps all four details it dropped: `format` and `compression` go into `data_body`'s `format` and `compression` fields; the `imageCollection_id` edge becomes a `member_of` relation to a group entity (T20); `data_limits` is left unwritten when it equals its data type's range ([intmin intmax] of the type, [0 1] for `logical`: every NDI `main` writer sets it so, and NDI's schema defines it as "The minimum and maximum value for that data type"), recoverable from the datum type ([lossless means round trip]), and is written as a "value range" condition on the body only when it differs. Replaces the drops in `image_stack.m` and `image.m`. Built (DID-matlab part): DID-matlab f6486be (#250). Built (did-schema part, the terms): did-schema c764a85 (EMPTY:0000402 value range registered). Built: DID-matlab f6486be (#250) (`image_stack.m` writes the EMPTY:0000402 value range); the `member_of` part is narrowed by [image collection carried until a writer], built DID-matlab 34deec0 (#305).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [probe contact geometry carried]: ("10a", drop census question 10) a v1 `probe_geometry` that sets any of `shank_id`, the contact shape and its width / height / radius, `manufacturer`, `has_planar_contour` or the contour is carried whole as v1 until V2 has a contact-geometry model; `probe_geometry.m` dropped them as "still deferred". None is set in today's corpora. Built: DID-matlab f6486be (#250)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [small unread settings]: ("a, record 12 that way"; 2-4 "ok", drop census question 12) (1) a v1 `sorting_parameters`, `spike_extraction_parameters(_modification)` or `vmspikefilteringparameters` whose `app` block names an OS or interpreter is carried whole as v1. That OS is where the settings were SAVED (NDI `ndi.app/newdocument` stamps every app document), not a property of the recipe: V2 puts a run's OS and interpreter on the CALCULATION (`operating_system_id`, `interpreter_id`), and `method` is NOT to gain OS or interpreter links on this account. (2) a filter's `sampleFrequency` becomes a method parameter; a fractional order, or a `label` that differs from its `type`, carries the filter as v1. (3) `syncrule` settings the migrator does not read (e.g. `errorOnFailure`) become method parameters; one that cannot be written carries the rule as v1. (4) an `element_epoch` whose `t0_t1` does not match its clocks, or is missing or not numeric, is carried as v1; this also removes the start-0/end-0 clocks `element_epoch.m` invents today ([no invented values]). None occurs in today's corpora. Built: DID-matlab f6486be (#250)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [carry register]: ("yes", to Claude's proposal after "How do we generally prevent this issue from happening to all of the passthrough cases?") every reason the generic migration carries a v1 document whole as v1 is a row in ONE carry register in did-schema, so that a carry is never read as "V2 is missing a field". Each row names the v1 class and field, the decision tag, and its KIND: `awaiting_home` (V2 should model it; names the open-work row), `not_modelled` (V2 models it elsewhere or not at all, by design; carries a "do not" line, e.g. "do not add OS links to `method`: V2 records a run's environment on the calculation"), or `malformed` (input no writer produces). Enforced both ways: every carry note a migrator writes cites its register row, and a DID-matlab test fails on a carry whose reason is not registered (generalising NDI's `verifyDeferralsAreDeclared`); a did-schema gate fails when a class gains a field or edge a `not_modelled` row forbids, quoting the "do not" line. The done report lists carried documents by kind. Built: did-schema #134 (the register and its gate), DID-matlab 1f83af39 (PR #251) (cite rows; strict citation check), NDI-matlab 38adc701b (PR #1074) (cite rows; the test)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [compression block and empty levels]: ("yes, record 11 that way", drop census question 11) (1) `data_body.compression` (today `char`, a codec name only) becomes a `{term, parameters}` block, the shape `method` uses: the codec as a term, its settings (e.g. Blosc's inner compressor, level, shuffle, block size) as parameters, so that no body loses its write-time settings; a light-sheet level's `codec_params` go there. A field type change, so DID-matlab and NDI-matlab readers and writers change first; until then a level whose settings go beyond the codec name is carried whole as v1 (carry register, `awaiting_home`). (2) a `lightsheetZarrLevel` holding no bytes becomes a file-less `sampled_body` that keeps the level's id, dimensions and metadata and says no bytes are stored, as [no-source pyramid gets a file-less store body]; this replaces #73 L3(c) ("A level holding NO bytes -> nothing"), which left edges to the level orphaned. None occurs in today's corpora. Built (DID-matlab part): DID-matlab f6486be (#250). Partly built: DID-matlab ef717a88 (PR #319) (readers take both shapes; the writer writes the block once the schema declares it). Partly built: NDI-matlab 7f175b6c (#1110) (reads the char and the block). Build pending: did-schema (declare the block; codec and parameter terms)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [spelling is notation]: ("yes, record it that way", on drop census question 14a) (1) `source_unit` stays a bound term ([source unit], 2026-10-21), not free text: its job is to make `source_value` machine-readable. (2) The test of [lossless means round trip] reads: V2 can be migrated back to a v1 document that states the same things; equivalent spellings of one registered term ("Hz", "spikes/s" -> om:hertz) are the same thing, and everything else must be recoverable exactly. (3) The spellings a migrator maps to a registered term are recorded in the term registry as that term's known synonyms, so the mapping is documented and reversible as a set; an unrecognised spelling stays as the term's name with an empty node and is reported, as today. For `jResponseVariable` (tuning `response_units`, decisions [response variable], [dF/F as variable], [unstated response variable]) this keeps today's mapping and adds the synonyms. Built (did-schema part): did-schema ccd05db (a `synonyms` column on every term registry row, `check_term_registry` rule R14, `propose_terms` reads it; filled with DID-matlab `jResponseVariable.m`'s spellings on om:hertz and EMPTY:0000397). Built: DID-matlab 260bdfbe (#321) (a recognised spelling converts and round-trips as equivalent).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [Haley logic leaves the generic migration]: ("14b, this is haley import specific and shouldn't be here at all. I'm not worried about haley-specific migration since we already have the v2 reimport written") DID-matlab `did2.convert.resolveLawnPlateSubjects` (the JH lawn / plate subjects and the C. elegans patch relabel to `exp/<e>/plate/<p>/patch/<q>`) is Haley-import logic and is removed from the generic migration; the Haley dataset's V2 shape comes from its V2 re-import (`ndi.setup.conv.haley.import_V2`), and the generic stage carries what it cannot place as v1 ([generic stage is lossless]). Proposed (not decided): the other Haley-specific pieces the 2026-10-24 audit lists leave with it -- DID `ontology_table_row.m`'s patch-geometry map (audit B2) and NDI `imagedEntitySubjects`'s plate join (audit B6). Built (DID-matlab part): DID-matlab f6486be (#250). Built (NDI-matlab part): NDI-matlab 9b99061 (#1072). Built: DID-matlab 888f1415 (#298) (audit B2: every table row carried whole, [table rows carried as v1]; no patch-geometry map left in `ontology_table_row.m`); NDI-matlab 9b99061 (#1072) (audit B6: `imagedEntitySubjects` retired; only comments name it).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [dataset-specific logic leaves the generic migration]: ("yes, all dataset-specific should move out of the generic migration") every piece of the generic migration (DID-matlab `did2.convert`, NDI-matlab `ndi.migrate.local` and its second pass) that encodes one dataset's meaning moves to that dataset's import or map; the generic stage places what it can by rules that hold for any dataset and carries the rest as v1. Known so far: DID `resolveLawnPlateSubjects` ([Haley logic leaves the generic migration]), DID `ontology_table_row.m`'s patch-geometry map (2026-10-24 audit B2), NDI `imagedEntitySubjects`'s plate join (audit B6), and NDI's Dabrowska slice chain (`sliceTetanus`; [sliceTetanus to Dab map]). The rest are found by an audit of both repositories, brought to the team as a list. Built (DID-matlab part): DID-matlab f6486be (#250). Built (NDI-matlab part): NDI-matlab 9b99061 (#1072). Build pending: DID-matlab, NDI-matlab (audit first)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [site scaffolding not a loss]: ("14c. agreed on your 2 suggested points", drop census question 14c) (1) NDI `pathSPromotion` deleting the `Anatomic Site` observation is not a loss: DID's pass 1 (`treatment.m` and its siblings) mints that observation with a NEW id as scaffolding beside the manipulation, which keeps the v1 id; the region term survives on the region part the manipulation is retargeted to ([region parts]), so the v1 treatment is recoverable ([lossless means round trip]). (2) Open work, after the lossless builds: pass 1 writes the final shape directly (manipulation, region part entity, its anatomical-structure assertion, `part_of` relation, anchor), the part's id COMPUTED from the subject id, region term and side so that every document naming one region of one subject yields the same part, and the batch pass only merges identical copies; a side stated in a separate v1 document still needs the batch pass. DID has no computed ids today (a new convention). Build pending: DID-matlab, NDI-matlab (open work, after the lossless builds)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [table rows into the dataset audit]: ("ok", drop census question 14d) NDI `ontologyRowSubjects` / `imagedEntitySubjects` and the DID per-table maps they call are taken up by the audit of [dataset-specific logic leaves the generic migration], which brings back: which per-table maps are dataset-specific; what a generic table row becomes (proposed: one statement per ontology-tagged column); and the `SubjectLocalIdentifier` label match (on by default, a "TEAM QUESTION" in the code with no decision), with counts of rows that rely on it. Built: DID-matlab f6486be (#250), NDI-matlab 9b99061 (#1072) (the audit's batches, [dataset audit batch 1 and 2]); then DID-matlab 888f1415 (#298), NDI-matlab 8a193422f (#1098) ([table rows carried as v1], which supersedes `ontologyRowSubjects`; no call to it remains in NDI-matlab).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [device clocks on one epoch]: ("mostly agreed", drop census questions 6 and 13, with her follow-up "the epoch gets two relative time references which link to each device entity?" -- yes) an epoch is ONE RECORDING, which may hold files from several acquisition systems (v1 NDI: each system's file navigator lists the epoch; with a per-folder navigator, systems reading one folder share the epoch id, e.g. Soph "t00001" on vhintan and vhvis_spike2). (1) One V2 epoch per recording (session + epoch id), as `epochMint` merges today. (2) Each system's extent is its own `relative_time_reference` on the epoch (`time_reference_id`), `value.clock` e.g. `dev_local_time`, its `referent_id` THAT ACQUISITION SYSTEM's entity -- not the session, as `epochMint` anchors today; the device entities gain nothing. (3) A v1 `syncrule_mapping` becomes a `clock_alignment` between those two references, so Soph's 348 non-identity mappings (e.g. scale 1.0000024, offset -4.33 s) and Dab's 2,484 identity mappings survive; its endpoint `objectname` / `objectclass` are left unwritten only when they equal the referent system, and `epochprobemap` only when it equals the epoch's manifest, else the mapping is carried as v1 ([lossless means round trip]). (4) Two sources disagreeing about ONE system's clock extent is malformed and carries the second as v1. Amends: the `recording epoch` entity type definition (binding registry, EMPTY:0000383) to "one recording: the files written for one stretch of time, by one or more acquisition systems"; and [time_reference uniqueness rule] (2026-09-30) so that one document's time references are unique by clock AND referent. To check in the build: that every acquisition epoch document can be tied to its acquisition system (an element epoch through its probe's device string, e.g. "vhvis_spike2:mk1"). Built (schema part, partly): did-schema 02e2980 (#131: recording epoch description; the clock-and-referent rule documented, machine-readable referent_unique_by waits for DID-matlab). Built (DID-matlab part): DID-matlab f6486be (#250); DID-matlab d2c9c1e4 (#284: silentLoss and timeReferenceFamilies read a composite `referent_unique_by`, clock then referent). Built (the uniqueness rule: `referent_unique_by: ["value.clock", "referent_id"]` on the three time_reference families): did-schema df75e00. Built: DID-matlab b517d5c7 (#250) (`epochMint.m` `deviceClockIndex`, `+migrators_j/private/jClockAlignmentBodies.m`); did-schema bf6bb62 (#161) (the EMPTY:0000383 definition: "One continuous stretch of recording, whose files may be written by one or more acquisition systems.").

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [clock terms get nodes]: ("yes, add it to the plan") the four `value.clock` values of `relative_time_reference` (`utc`, `dev_local_time`, `dev_global_time`, `exp_global_time`; a closed, required list bound to `did_clocktype`) get real nodes: an existing ontology term where one fits, else EMPTY terms minted in empty-ontology (T19); today every node is "" ("STAGED EMPTY"). Done with the other term additions the drop census needs ([session membership records linked or ingested], [dataset form terms], [image file details placed]). Built (terms registered): empty-ontology 25188df (#26), did-schema 02e2980 (#131); the binding's value names wait for DID-matlab and NDI-matlab to compare nodes. Built: DID-matlab f510a050 (#314), NDI-matlab 86038a21 (#1106) (clock nodes written and compared first). Built: DID-matlab 41b66219 (#328), NDI-matlab 5b50ba7d (#1117) (their tests compare the clock by node and admit the old name or the label). Built: did-schema ac7b596 (the binding's value names are the term labels, with their nodes; the terms are registered, above)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [writer default is not set]: ("probe geometry question 1: answer a") a v1 field whose value equals the v1 writer's own fixed default counts as not set ([lossless means round trip]: the default is recoverable), so it does not by itself carry a document as v1. First case: `probe_geometry.has_planar_contour` (NDI template default 1, `fromStruct` writes 0), which today would carry every writer-shaped probe geometry under [probe contact geometry carried]. Each default relied on is cited to its NDI `main` writer. Built: DID-matlab f6486be (#250)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [one correct reading]: ("record the general rule and the new register kind") the generic migration writes a V2 document only when the v1 document by itself, with rules that hold for every dataset, gives EXACTLY ONE correct V2 reading; a V2 document whose meaning might be wrong (e.g. a medium the worms live on written as a dose) is never written. When the v1 document admits several readings it is carried whole as v1 with a [carry register] row of the new kind `needs_mapping`: the row names the V2 home(s), what decides between them, and the dataset map entry that resolves it (e.g. "Babu: MICRO:0000480 -> exposure"), which the generic tools then apply, so a carry never reads as "V2 has no home". The register kinds are `awaiting_home`, `needs_mapping`, `not_modelled`, `malformed`. Answers already given are to be re-checked against this rule (Jess: "Does that change any other answers ... WE should double check"). Built (DID-matlab part): DID-matlab f6486be (#250). Built: did-schema a5dc513 (#134) (the carry register and its kind `needs_mapping`); the re-check is [one-reading quick answers], built DID-matlab f6486be (#250); NDI-matlab 9b99061 (#1072) (the second pass's dataset logic out, rows carried rather than guessed, [dataset audit batch 1 and 2]).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [run environment on interactions]: ("A. a") the two optional run-environment edges `operating_system_id` and `interpreter_id` (today on `calculation`, [calculation-os-optional]) move up to `interaction`, so a manipulation and an observation have them too: a v1 `stimulus_presentation`'s `app` OS and interpreter are the machine the stimulus program ran on, and become software entities on those edges (1,428 presentations, drop census). Not for `method` ([small unread settings]). Built: did-schema 02e2980 (#131), NDI-matlab 9b99061 (#1072)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [control pairing is a calculation]: ("yes, record the control record that way", after her question whether the control is "truly calculated after the manipulation") which stimuli are controls is a stimulus parameter known before stimulation (default `isblank` = 1, already on the V2 stimulus `method` documents); what NDI's `tuning_response` `control_stimulus` computes afterwards is the PAIRING of each presented stimulus with its control trial, by a rule (default "pseudorandom": the `controlid` stimulus in the same repetition; NDI-matlab main `+app/+stimulus/tuning_response.m` 551-658). A v1 `control_stimulus_ids` therefore becomes a `calculation` about the presentation: software `ndi_app_tuning_response` (from its `app`), method = the pairing rule (`control_stimulus_id_method`: method, controlid, controlid_value, verbatim), value = the per-trial pairing. Replaces [control selection on the presentation method] (the rule is that program's setting, not the presentation's) and amends [control as covariate]: the per-trial pairing is kept, not dropped; the `control condition` covariate may stay as a convenience (recoverable from `isblank`). Built (NDI-matlab part): NDI-matlab 9b99061 (#1072). Built: NDI-matlab 9b99061 (#1072) (`local.m` folds a v1 `control_stimulus_ids` into a `calculation` with the same id; `stimulusPresentationToItem.m` writes the pairing method; no term was named as missing).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [census terms]: ("T1 yes, T2 b, T3 yes, T4 yes"; proposal `docs/v2_working/TERMS_PROPOSAL.md`, Claude, 2026-10-26) T1: two new EMPTY terms "database ingestion" and "database linking" for the session membership method ([session membership records linked or ingested]); a linked session's path is NCIT:C47922 Pathname; its opener class is a new EMPTY "software class". T2: the dataset form variables are NCIT:C16847 Technique and NCIT:C175886 Scientific Data Type ([dataset form terms]); their values are openMINDS controlled terms identified by openMINDS's own stable ids under a newly registered `openminds:` prefix (no EMPTY minting; 227 techniques, 4 semantic data types; Soph's "derivedData" is openMINDS semanticDataType derivedData). T3: a new EMPTY "value range" for [image file details placed] (NCIT:C38013 Range leads with a single difference). T4: the clock values get nodes ([clock terms get nodes]): `utc` -> wikidata:Q1536; new EMPTY "device local time", "device global time", "experiment global time" (definitions from NDI `clocktype.m`); the stored name becomes the term label, so DID-matlab and NDI-matlab compare clock nodes first before the schema changes. Built (the terms): empty-ontology 25188df (#26: EMPTY:0000399 database ingestion, 0000400 database linking, 0000401 software class, 0000402 value range, 0000403-0000405 the three clocks), did-schema 02e2980 (#131: `openminds:` prefix, registry rows, bindings). Built (DID-matlab part): DID-matlab f6486be (#250). Built: DID-matlab f6486be (#250) (T1 membership terms, `jMembershipBodies.m`; T2 `metadata_editor.m`), f510a050 (#314), 41b66219 (#328) (T4 clock nodes); NDI-matlab 915bbb3 (#1103) (T1 membership terms), 86038a21 (#1106), 5b50ba7d (#1117) (T4 clock nodes).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [dataset audit batch 1 and 2]: ("yes to haley and babu"; "yes to all 3"; dataset-specific audit `docs/v2_working/DATASET_SPECIFIC_AUDIT.md`) under [dataset-specific logic leaves the generic migration] and [one correct reading]: (1) the Haley worm-track shapes (`resolveEpochProbemap.m` position/distance cases, stemKey, endpoint B, pixel frame) leave the generic migration -- such epochs stay plain acquisition epochs or carry as v1, and the Haley V2 import makes the tracks; this supersedes [JH position and distance] and [JH build choices] for the generic stage; (2) the Babu media/air/heat routing (`jTreatmentRoute.m` isMedium, `treatment_drug.m` isHeatOnly) leaves; those rows carry as v1 with a `needs_mapping` row naming exposure / temperature manipulation, superseding [babu-media] for the generic stage; (3) `ontologyRowSubjects` is built to match [subject local identifier is an identifier] (the document id wins when routes disagree), keeping the local-identifier label match (Dabrowska's only route); (4) table columns and `treatment` rows whose kind, direction or unit is chosen from label words carry as v1 with `needs_mapping` rows instead of a guessed reading; (5) NDI `imagedEntitySubjects` is retired. Built (DID-matlab part): DID-matlab f6486be (#250). Built (NDI-matlab part): NDI-matlab 9b99061 (#1072). Built: DID-matlab f6486be (#250) ((1) `stemKey` and endpoint B gone from `resolveEpochProbemap.m`; (2) `isMedium` and `isHeatOnly` gone), NDI-matlab 9b99061 (#1072) ((5) `imagedEntitySubjects` retired); (3) and (4) by [table rows carried as v1], DID-matlab 888f1415 (#298), NDI-matlab 8a193422f (#1098).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [term-stated unit is generic]: ("sure if the term lists a unit, we can derive the unit explicitly") when a term's definition states the unit its accompanying number takes, the generic migration derives that unit for any dataset: the unit is stated on the term's row in the term registry (not in migrator code), and a migrator reads it from there. First case: EMPTY:0000199 "treatment: grating direction-of-motion visual stimulation", defined "... (provided in hours)" (DID-matlab `treatment.m` termNumberKind). The v1 schema does not state it (NDI `treatment_schema.json`: numeric_value "The numeric value associated with a treatment"). Supersedes [termNumberKind is per-dataset]. Built (schema part): did-schema 02e2980 (#131: registry `number_unit`, rule R12; EMPTY:0000199 = om:hour). Built: DID-matlab f6486be (#250), did-schema 02e2980 (#131)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [software_id is what produced the statement]: ("1 yes", after she found separate "recorded by" links duplicative) an interaction's `software_id` means one thing everywhere: the software that PRODUCED this statement -- for a calculation the program that computed it, for an acquired observation the program that acquired it, for a decoded stimulus presentation the decoder that produced the playlist from the stimulus files (NDI-matlab main `decoder.m` 135-137 stamps its `app`). The run-environment edges ([run environment on interactions]) describe that same run. So [presentation app is software] and [run environment on interactions] stand, read as "the program that produced the record" (not "the program that presented the stimulus"); no new links. A settings document's saved-on OS stays carried as v1 ([small unread settings] (1)). Built: did-schema 02e2980 (#131), NDI-matlab 9b99061 (#1072)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [stimulus_presentation retired]: ("2a retire") `stimulus_presentation` (stable/, in_progress, superclasses base/app/epochid) is a v1 shape, not a V2 class: its V2 form is the decided decomposition (a `manipulation` with value kind `item` plus `method` documents, NDI second pass). Its disposition becomes `retire`; DID's pass 1 carries a v1 `stimulus_presentation` unchanged as v1 ([passthrough stays v1]) and NDI's second pass reads the v1 document. Removes one of the two in-progress inheritors of retired `epochid` ([retired classes leave V2]). Built (schema part): did-schema 02e2980 (#131: disposition retire). Built (DID-matlab part): DID-matlab f6486be (#250). Built: DID-matlab f6486be (#250) (pass 1 hands the v1 document on: `v1_to_v2.m` `handedOn`), NDI-matlab a429e6009 (#1021) (`+migrate/+internal/stimulusPresentationToItem.m` reads the v1 document).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [ingested stimulus file is a body of the presentation]: ("2b i") a v1 `daqmetadatareader_epochdata_ingested` (NDI-matlab main `+daq/metadatareader.m` ingest_epochfiles 126-145: the stored copy of an epoch's stimulus file, e.g. NewStim `stims.mat` read by `ndi.daq.metadatareader.NewStimStims`, as compressed `data.bin`, with the epoch id and a `daqmetadatareader_id` edge; Dab 1,242, Soph 175, 20211116 0 held) becomes an `opaque_body` owned by that epoch's stimulus-presentation `manipulation` (the raw source the playlist was decoded from), joined by session and epoch id in NDI's second pass; with no matching presentation it is carried as v1. Its class disposition becomes `retire`, the second in-progress inheritor of retired `epochid` ([retired classes leave V2]). Built (schema part): did-schema 02e2980 (#131: disposition retire). Built (DID-matlab part): DID-matlab f6486be (#250). Built (NDI-matlab part): NDI-matlab 9b99061 (#1072). Built: DID-matlab f6486be (#250) (pass 1 hands it on as v1: `v1_to_v2.m` `handedOn`), NDI-matlab 9b99061 (#1072) (`+migrate/+internal/ingestedStimulusBodies.m`, joined by session and epoch id).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [one-reading quick answers]: ("Q1–Q5 yes"; one-reading review `docs/v2_working/ONE_READING_REVIEW.md`) (Q1) a treatment with no recorded location, or a non-body location, is carried as v1 instead of becoming a dose with a NaN amount; (Q2) a `treatment_transfer` with no clock type, or a bath epoch with no clock record, is carried as v1 instead of defaulting to `dev_local_time` ([no invented values]), and a global-clock time is written in its true unit (NDI stores days, not seconds); (Q3) JH `imageStack` / `ontologyLabel` readings chosen from label words (mask or map detection, every label as an observation) carry as `needs_mapping` ([one correct reading]); (Q4) the Dab treatment name "Optogenetic Tetanus Stimulation Target Location" is kept unchanged (the fold no longer strips "Target Location"), and its term EMPTY:0000074 is registered in the term registry; (Q5) a `jrclust_clusters` with an `ndi_document.name` (e.g. "jrclust.prm") stays carried as v1 until it has a home. Built (schema part): did-schema 02e2980 (#131: EMPTY:0000074 registered). Built (DID-matlab part): DID-matlab f6486be (#250). Built: DID-matlab f6486be (#250; Q1 `jTreatmentRoute.m`/`jTreatmentBodies.m`, Q2 `jTreatmentAnchor.m`, Q3 `image_stack.m`, Q4 `treatment.m`, Q5 `jrclust_clusters.m`). Built (did-schema part): carry-157 (needs_mapping), the row the bath's carry cites. Built: DID-matlab a78299d8 (#325) (`resolveDeferredBaths.m` `stimulatorClock` no longer defaults a bath with no clock record to `dev_local_time`; it carries citing carry-157)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [carry register kinds]: ("That's fine", to the 13 carry register rows whose kind Claude read from their decision lines, listed on did-schema PR #134, and to filing OPEN_WORK rows for the `awaiting_home` rows that had none) the kinds stand as read: `malformed` for carry-006 (`filter`, fractional order), carry-008 (`syncrule`, a setting that cannot be written), carry-009 (`element_epoch`, `t0_t1` not matching its clocks, missing or not numeric), carry-014 (`hartley_calc`, `stimulus_properties` differing from the generator settings), carry-016 (`calculator`, the inner `depends_on` copy differing), carry-017 (`daqreader_ndr`, the reader class differing from the reader string), carry-018 (`neuron_extracellular`, counts differing from the waveform) and carry-019 (`lightsheetZarrPyramid`, no source file and no dtype); `awaiting_home` for carry-007 (`filter.label`), carry-011 and carry-012 (`image` / `imageStack` `data_limits`), carry-013 (`imageStack` `imageCollection_id`) and carry-015 (`hartley_calc.dimension_labels`). The register marks them `kind_basis: decided`. OPEN_WORK #130 (carry-001, issue #135), #131 (carry-007, issue #136) and #132 (carry-010, issue #137) are filed.

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [awaiting home is tracked]: ("Yes, you can file those"; "You can make the register check fail when missing the open work entry") every `awaiting_home` row of the carry register names an OPEN_WORK row, and `tools/check_carry_register.py` FAILS a row with none, or whose row is not in the "## OPEN" section of `did_v2_OPEN_WORK.md` (C5), instead of listing it. The four rows that had none got OPEN_WORK #133 (carry-011, carry-012; issue #138), #134 (carry-013; issue #139) and #135 (carry-015; issue #140).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [dataset map edits V2]: ("b", to Claude's question whether a dataset map (a) only converts documents the generic stage carried as v1, or (b) may also edit V2 documents the generic stage wrote) a dataset map runs AFTER the generic migration, which stays dataset-free and converts every document that has one correct reading exactly as today; the map then adds what only that dataset knows, and may change V2 documents the generic stage wrote (e.g. Dab: mint the slices, add the tetanus runs, move the baths and statements from the animal to its slice, drop the before/after labels), as well as convert carried v1 documents. Every change comes from a map row that cites the decision behind it. Build pending: NDI-matlab (the map step and the Dab map, [sliceTetanus to Dab map])

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [dataset map location]: ("i think your map plan sounds good", to Claude's proposal `docs/v2_working/DATASET_MAP_PROPOSAL.md`, option C) each dataset's map is one file in NDI-matlab, beside that dataset's importer and decision log, and is applied by its own step after the generic migration has written the V2 database, so a map can be re-run after an edit without migrating again; the generic second pass gets no dataset slots. Build pending: NDI-matlab (the map step, the Dab map)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [no Haley map]: ("a", to Claude's question whether the Haley dataset needs a dataset map) Haley gets no dataset map: its V2 data comes from the Haley V2 import, which reads the source data, not the v1 documents ([Haley logic leaves the generic migration]). A generic migration of Haley's v1 documents may carry some of them, and nothing resolves those carries.

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [map rows match exact values]: ("A", to Claude's question whether a dataset map row may match on words in a label or only on exact v1 values) a dataset map row matches only exact v1 values (a term node, a class, a field value); it never matches on words in a label. A document no row matches exactly stays as the generic migration left it. Build pending: NDI-matlab (the map step)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [opto target without a run]: ("B", to Claude's question what the Dab map does with an animal that has an "Optogenetic Tetanus Stimulation Target Location" treatment (EMPTY:0000074) but no tetanus run found in its recordings) the Dab map records the stated target as a term assertion on the animal ("its stimulation target was region X"), claiming no stimulation; today's `sliceTetanus` keeps the old manipulation on the animal instead (its `treatment_manipulations_kept_no_run` count). Build pending: NDI-matlab (the Dab map)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [fraction value kind]: ("yes, add fraction", after asking "Why fraction instead of score?") V2 gains a `fraction` value kind for a part of a whole (e.g. the share of a test's time spent in an arm). Its canonical value is a plain ratio, 1 = the whole, in `om:one`; a percent is kept losslessly as `source_value` with `source_unit` `om:percent`. It is not a `score`: a score is a rating on a named rubric (`scale`, `scale_min`, `scale_max`), a fraction is a ratio that means the same in every dataset. Built: did-schema `schemas/did_v2/value/fraction.json` (this commit)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [migrators read raw v1]: ("yes, record both", after "What's the best design for a migrator? I feel like it's c") a migrator reads v1 exactly as v1 defines it (the NDI template and writer at the locked `main`), writes V2 only through the builder, and accounts for every v1 field; what is truly shared (`depends_on`, the `base` and `app` blocks, the stamp) becomes reading helpers each migrator calls on the raw v1 document, not a pass that rewrites it. Migrators switch one at a time, each with a check that every v1 template field is accounted for; `did2.convert.universalRenames` is deleted once the last one has switched; one full corpus run at the end, batched with the other sibling renames. OPEN_WORK #136. Partly built: DID-matlab e73aec7 (PR #253) (the helpers `+did2/+convert/+v1read/` and `+did2/+convert/+migrators_j/private/jRawBody.m`, `jRawAccount.m`, `jRawCarry.m`; 72 of the 95 `+migrators_j` migrators read raw v1, listed in `+did2/+convert/rawV1Classes.m`, each with a round trip); DID-matlab 6bfbb25 (PR #255) (contrast_tuning, spatial_frequency_tuning, temporal_frequency_tuning, speed_tuning: 76 of 95); DID-matlab dfd73b6 (PR #256) (pyraview: 77 of 95); DID-matlab 63c1ba1 (PR #257) (file_reference, lightsheet_zarr_level, lightsheet_zarr_pyramid: 80 of 95). DID-matlab 9edf8991 (PR #315) (control_stimulus_ids, image). DID-matlab 14cf1823 (PR #324) (the last 9: dataset_session_info, fitcurve, ontology_table_row, openminds, openminds_element, openminds_stimulus, openminds_subject, vmspikefilteringparameters, vmspikefit). Build pending: DID-matlab (delete `universalRenames`, which still runs for v1 classes with no `+migrators_j` migrator (DID-matlab #315 lists them), still called at `v1_to_v2.m:391` and `:1271`; then the one full corpus run)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [renames carry is temporary]: ("yes, record both") the carry "universalRenames would discard" ends with [migrators read raw v1]: until the pass is deleted it is one carry register row for any class (`v1_class` `*`, carry-049, awaiting_home on OPEN_WORK #136), removed when the pass is deleted; a collision a raw-v1 migrator still meets becomes a class-specific row. Built: did-schema (carry-049; `check_carry_register.py` accepts `*`, this commit). Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-31 [classes without a migrator carry as v1]: ("Ok", to "#143: the 9 v1 classes with no migrator are kept unchanged as v1. OK? The classes are animalsubject, demoNDISeries, demoNDISeriesMixed, ensemble, generic_file, imageCollection, projectvar, stimulus_parameter_table and valid_interval. Each would get a carry row, and a migrator can replace that row later.") a v1 class with no migrator is carried whole as v1, citing its own carry register row, instead of going through `universalRenames`; a migrator written later replaces its row. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md #143 Q2 (did-schema #143, OPEN_WORK #136). Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [early migration pieces fixed]: ("I think we should fix all of these", to Claude's list of migration pieces from early V2 development) (1) superclass migrators and the old `+migrators` generation go: one migrator per class (with [migrators read raw v1]), and a class with no migrator is a recorded decision, not an identity fallback; (2) `ensureClassBlocks` padding goes: the builder produces a complete document; (3) the `TermRegistry` strict check stays on during migration (DID-matlab `v1_to_v2.m`, `epochMint.m`), an unregistered term becoming a carry with a register row or a registry row; (4) "already converted" is a stamp check (`did_v1` / `did_v2`), not a rank against `V_eta`; (5) element name and reference are stored reversibly (OPEN_WORK #142; NDI `+vintage/elementFields.m` packs them as '%s (ref %s)'); (6) the uncalled `resolveLawnPlateSubjects` and its mentions are deleted from DID-matlab ([dataset-specific logic leaves the generic migration]). Built: DID-matlab 1f83af39 (PR #251) (4: `+did2/+convert/isAlreadyTarget.m`, commit 00a87879; 6: `resolveLawnPlateSubjects` deleted, commit ad66d7bc). Partly built: DID-matlab e73aec7 (PR #253) (1: the 72 classes in `rawV1Classes.m` run one migrator each, with no superclass migrator; old `+migrators` wrappers nothing reached are deleted). Build pending: DID-matlab (1: the 23 classes not switched still reach `+did2/+convert/+migrators/` (`element_epoch`, `epochclocktimes`, `filter`, `ngrid`, and the `identity` fallback, `v1_to_v2.m:124`) and `+did2/+convert/+migrators_j/+super/` (`image_stack_parameters`, `ngrid`); 2: `ensureClassBlocks` still pads every converted body, `v1_to_v2.m:507`; 3: `TermRegistry` is still off during migration, `v1_to_v2.m:159`, `epochMint.m:396`), NDI-matlab (5), did-schema (5: the schema shape, once decided)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [migration reliability practices]: ("Do all 4", to Claude's proposal) (a) a round-trip check per class: each converted document is migrated back to v1 and compared with its source, built with each class's switch to raw v1 ([migrators read raw v1]); (b) deterministic ids: a document the migration mints gets an id derived from its v1 source id and its role, so two runs on the same input are identical and can be diffed; (c) whole-dataset passes (DID-matlab `resolve*` post-passes, NDI-matlab's second pass) declare what they read and write, and their order is derived and checked, not kept in comments; (d) the migration report counts carries by carry register row, with its denominator. Partly built: DID-matlab 1f83af39 (PR #251) (b: `+did2/+convert/mintId.m`), e73aec7 (PR #253) (a: `+did2/+convert/+roundtrip/<class>.m` and `roundtrip.check` for the 72 classes that read raw v1, 22 `testRawV1RoundTrip<Family>` suites; b: `+did2/+convert/mintKey.m`, a document with no `base` block). Build pending: DID-matlab (a: the 23 classes not switched; b: ids are still random where a source states no id, `did.ido.unique_id()` at `mintId.m:83` and `:102`, `resolveEpochProbemap.m:1574`, `resolveOpenmindsCitations.m:1028`, `+did2/+convert/+migrators_j/private/jDatasetId.m:17`, `pyraview.m:97`, `image_stack.m:346`, `jrclust_clusters.m:107`; c; d), NDI-matlab (c, d)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [carry register kinds 2]: ("1 yes", to Claude's list of the kinds it proposed for carry register rows carry-027 to carry-048 and carry-050 to carry-056) those kinds stand as proposed; the register marks them `kind_basis: decided`. (carry-034, `subject_group`, is reworded by the answer on unnamed groups.)

Each entity including groups should probably be required to have a user facing name, right?"; replaces her earlier "2 b") the `Group` entity type keeps requiring a `local_identifier`, its user-facing name; a v1 `subject_group` states none (NDI's template block is empty), so the generic migration carries it whole as v1 (DID-matlab `subject_group.m`, unchanged) as a `needs_mapping` carry (carry-034), and the dataset map gives the group its name, after which it converts with its `member_of` edges. Nothing is filled in by the generic stage: not a generated handle, not the document id ([no invented values]). Checked at the same time: every one of the 19 entity types already requires a user-facing name, `local_identifier` (dataset-local: organism, culture, tissue, cell, Group, device, material entity, Session, recording epoch) or `name` (shared: the other ten) (`binding_registry_meta.json` `entity_type_bindings`). Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [ingested session membership]: ("1. Yes", to Claude's proposal) for an INGESTED session, a v1 `session_in_a_dataset`'s `session_reference` is dropped when it equals the session entity's `local_identifier` (recoverable from it), and the creator class becomes a parameter of the "database ingestion" method on the session's `part_of` relation ([session membership records linked or ingested]); when `session_reference` differs, the document is carried as v1, `needs_mapping` (carry-060). Built: DID-matlab 1f83af39 (PR #251) (`jMembershipBodies.m`)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [data_limits not a pair is malformed]: ("2. A") a valid `data_limits` [min max] pair is written as a value range ([image file details placed]), so carry-011 and carry-012 now catch only a `data_limits` that is not a finite [min max] pair: they become `malformed`, and OPEN_WORK #133 is completed.

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [pass-through filter sample frequency]: ("2. A", conditional on Claude checking that the body's own sample rate always matches) the `sampleFrequency` an all-pass pyraview filter states is not carried when it equals the pyraview's `native_rate`, which the V2 body keeps (`dt = 1/native_rate`): it is recoverable from it. Checked: NDI's writer sets both from one variable (NDI-matlab main `+gui/+app/+pyraview/makePyraviewDoc.m:53` `sr = probe.samplerate(epochid)`, `:58` `filterData([0], sr, filterband)`, `:157` `pyraviewStruct.nativeRate = sr`; `filterData.m:37` `sampleFrequency`, sr). A value that differs is carried as v1, `malformed` (carry-061). Built: DID-matlab 1f83af39 (PR #251) (`jFrequencyFilter.m`, `pyraview.m`)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [untied epochs keep the session anchor]: ("2. Yes", to Claude's option under [device clocks on one epoch]) an element epoch that cannot be tied to an acquisition system (e.g. 20211116, which holds no epochprobemap: 0 of 252 tied) is not carried when all of its extents come from one untied source and agree: it keeps a session-anchored time reference, using only the times the epoch states. Extents that come from several sources, or disagree, still carry it. Built: DID-matlab 1f83af39 (PR #251) (`epochMint`), NDI-matlab d88ea6cfb (PR #1075, its test)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [sorted units are cells or groups]: ("A'", after asking whether the source of sorted spikes is cells, a recording unit or tissue) a sorted unit (v1 `element` of type `spikes`) is an entity derived from its electrode and its sorting run ([neuron derived from its sorting run]): a SINGLE unit is a `cell`; a MULTI-unit cluster is a `Group` of cells whose members are not identified (no `member_of` rows; its local_identifier is the unit's name). Whether it is single or multi is an assertion by the sorting run, so a re-sort changes an assertion, not what the entity is. The raw recording stays an observation of the specimen with the electrode as instrument (TEAM-SIGN-OFF [raw recording observation]); tissue is where the cells are (`part_of` / located in), not the source of a sorted spike train. A unit with no single/multi label is not decided here. Built: DID-matlab e73aec7 (PR #253) (`+did2/+convert/+migrators_j/element.m` `elementEntityType`, `:333-348`)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [unlabelled units need a map]: ("1. A") a sorted unit with no single/multi label (198 in the corpora: 134 Dab, 64 Soph, `scratchpad/elemtypes` census) is carried as v1, `needs_mapping` (carry-062): the dataset map says single or multi. Built: DID-matlab e73aec7 (PR #253) (`+did2/+convert/+migrators_j/element.m:344`, carry-062)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [element types have no default]: ("yes, draft the element type list"; "2. Yes"; "2a") an element's entity type comes from a fixed list of element types whose meaning NDI fixes, with NO default (today's `otherwise -> device` in DID-matlab `element.m` `elementEntityType` goes). A registered probe type (NDI `probetype2object.json`) is a `device` ONLY when the element's `ndi_element_class` is `ndi.probe.*`; a derived element that copies its probe's type (NDI `oneepoch.m:63-64`, `downsample.m:41-44`) is not. `spikes`, `neuron` and `cell` are sorted units ([sorted units are cells or groups]); `ensemble` is a `Group`. Any other type, or a probe type on a non-probe class, is carried as v1, `needs_mapping` (carry-063). The full list is the draft `docs/v2_working/ELEMENT_TYPES_DRAFT.md` (Proposed, not decided) until its open questions are answered. Built: DID-matlab e73aec7 (PR #253) (`+did2/+convert/+migrators_j/element.m` `elementEntityType`, `:305`; no default, `:349` carries any other type under carry-063)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [dependents of a carried document carry]: ("1A") a document whose edge points at a document carried as v1 (e.g. an `element_epoch`, `position_metadata` or spike train of a carried element) is carried too, down the chain, rather than converting with an edge to a v1 document (carry-064, any class). Built: DID-matlab e73aec7 (PR #253) (`+did2/+convert/v1_to_v2.m` `carryDependents`, `:1226`). Partly built: NDI-matlab 38adc701b (PR #1074) (a second-pass record blocked by a document carried as v1 counts under carry-064: `+ndi/+migrate/+internal/carryUnchangedAsV1.m:181`, `ontologyLabelSubjects.m:235`, `strainAssembly.m:309`, the control records in `+ndi/+migrate/local.m:2924`). Partly built: NDI-matlab 9e5e3030 (#1111) (a final sweep carries every document whose edge names a final v1 carry). Built: NDI-matlab 9e5e3030 (#1111) (`+migrate/+internal/carryFinalDependents.m`: every document not v1 at the end whose `depends_on` edge names a final v1 carry is carried; test `TestCarryFinalDependents.m`). The three cases #1111 lists (second-pass documents with no v1 source, `session_id` edges, folded inputs) are team questions, not this line.

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [electrode spellings and imaging types are devices]: ("1 a", "2a") on an element whose `ndi_element_class` is `ndi.probe.*` ([element types have no default]): (1) the spelled-out electrode forms (`electrode-<spec>`, `<prefix>_electrode-<spec>`, e.g. `electrode-4`, `extracellular_electrode-4`) map to `device`, like the registered key `electrode-$`, though NDI's `probestruct2probe` accepts only exact keys; (2) NDI's four imaging element types (`docs/v2_working/ELEMENT_TYPES_DRAFT.md`) map to `device`: the camera or microscope is the instrument and the image data is an observation made with it. Built: DID-matlab e73aec7 (PR #253) (`+did2/+convert/+migrators_j/element.m` `elementEntityType`: `:322` the electrode spellings, `:317-321` the four imaging types, `device` only on `ndi.probe.*` `:323-325`)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [event lines and environment probes are devices]: ("1a", "2a") on an `ndi.probe.*` class ([element types have no default]): an `event` element (a sync or trigger line, NDI's stimulator probe class) is a `device`, its pulses observations made with it; the environment probes `lick-spout`, `reward-well` and `intraoral-cannula` are `device`s (apparatus). Built: DID-matlab e73aec7 (PR #253) (`+did2/+convert/+migrators_j/element.m` `elementEntityType`, `:317-321`: `event`, `lick-spout`, `reward-well`, `intraoral-cannula`)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [migrator writes the registry spelling]: ("1a. Though I think maybe it's worth revisiting") the migrator writes an entity type exactly as the registry names it (`Group`, NCIT:C43359), not `group`. Open: whether NDI terms should be standardised to lower case (unless proper nouns) — as presentation only, or as an `ndi_name` registry column beside the ontology label (Proposed, not decided; `docs/V2_PLAN.md`). Built: DID-matlab 8552835 (#277: `+migrators_j/element.m` (multi-unit, ensemble) and `subject_group.m` write `Group`; tests assert the stored name and node)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [location carries need a map]: ("2. A") carry-023 and carry-024 (a treatment with no recorded location, or a location that is not an UBERON body site) are `needs_mapping`, now confirmed (their decision line, [one-reading quick answers] Q1, says only "carried as v1"); the register marks them `kind_basis: decided`.

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [presentation outside the batch needs a map]: ("3 b") a v1 `hartley_calc` whose presentation is not in the migrated batch is carried as `needs_mapping` (carry-065), not `malformed`: the document is sound and its presentation is elsewhere. carry-040 keeps the inputs that are malformed in themselves.

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [missing is_linked is malformed]: ("4 a") a v1 `session_in_a_dataset` with an empty `is_linked` is carried as `malformed` (carry-066): NDI's template leaves it empty (`session_in_a_dataset.json:16`, `"is_linked": []`) and its writer always sets it (`ndi.dataset` `dataset.m:92` `= 1` linked, `:170` `= 0` ingested), so a missing flag is not writer-shaped and a default would be invented. Built: DID-matlab 1f83af39 (PR #251) (`jMembershipBodies.m`)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [term labels stay as the ontology spells them]: ("Record the capitalization decision as you proposed", after asking what the genuinely better practice is) a term is identified by its node, never its label; the registry stores each term's label exactly as its source ontology spells it, so it can be checked and refreshed against the ontology (NCIT title-cases, e.g. `Group`; OBO ontologies use lower case); EMPTY's own terms follow the OBO convention, lower case except proper nouns and acronyms; any lower-casing for consistency is done where labels are DISPLAYED, never in what is stored. This settles the question [migrator writes the registry spelling] left open.

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [element local identifier is name and reference]: ("5 yes") a migrated element's `local_identifier` is NDI's own display form `<name> | <reference>` (NDI-matlab main `+ndi/element.m:341`, `[ndi_element_obj.name ' | ' int2str(ndi_element_obj.reference)]`), e.g. `leftcortex | 2`; the name alone is not unique (Soph holds `leftcortex` n-trodes with references 1, 2 and 3; 122 (name, type) pairs in Soph and 6 in Dab have more than one reference, census of the corpus zips, 2026-10-26). It is reversible: the reference is an integer, so splitting at the last ` | ` recovers both. Replaces the unescaped `'%s (ref %s)'` packing (OPEN_WORK #142). Built: DID-matlab e73aec7 (PR #253) (`+did2/+convert/+migrators_j/element.m:158`, `did2.convert.elementLocalIdentifier`). Built: NDI-matlab 4effe11e0 (PR #1076) (`+vintage/elementLocalIdentifier.m`, `elementNameReference.m`, `elementFields.m`, `elementDoc.m`; the old `(ref X)` form still reads)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [bath is two statements]: ("6 yes do both") a v1 bath record makes (1) in the generic stage, a manipulation "the subject sat in this solution": variable a general bath-solution term, value the whole formulation, written for every bath, and (2) in the dataset map, a separate manipulation of the ingredient that was really changed (e.g. Dab's 131 antagonist changes among its 1,172 ACSF baths), its variable that ingredient. The variable is no longer the first mixture row (e.g. "sodium chloride"). Partly built: DID-matlab 0f465e50 (#322), NDI-matlab 3c06d861 (#1112) (the bath variable is EMPTY:0000409 "bath solution"). Build pending: DID-matlab (the bath variable; term registry row for the general term), NDI-matlab (the Dab map)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [chooseLevel skips empty levels]: ("7 a") NDI's `chooseLevel` skips a pyramid level that stores no bytes (the file-less `sampled_body` of [compression block and empty levels]); today, on a voxel-size tie, it takes whichever comes last, which can be the empty level. Built: NDI-matlab 4effe11e0 (PR #1076) (`chooseLevel`, with tests)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [ingested stimulus reader finds the body]: ("8a") NDI's `ndi.daq.metadatareader.get_ingested_document` also finds a migrated ingested stimulus file as the presentation's data body, by session and epoch ([ingested stimulus file is a body of the presentation]), and reads the same `.nbf.tgz` file. Today it queries only the v1 shape (`depends_on daqmetadatareader_id` and `epochid.epochid`, NDI-matlab V2 `+daq/metadatareader.m:171-172`), and `readmetadata_ingested` returns `{}` without an error when nothing matches, so every stimulus-parameter read on a migrated ingested dataset would come back empty (from reading the code, not run). A test migrates an ingested epoch and reads its parameters back. Built: NDI-matlab 4effe11e0 (PR #1076) (`+daq/metadatareader.m`, `+vintage/ingestedStimulusKey.m`, test)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [markdown cleanup and rename]: ("9 yes", "10 ok", "11 ok, but can we rename the v eta docs v2?", "12 yes", "1 did_v2_, 2 yes rename now"; plan `docs/v2_working/MARKDOWN_PLAN.md`) under [markdown out of schemas]: (1) the 14 delete candidates go (the `V_alpha`..`V_zeta` specs and notes and `did_v2_notes.md`; git keeps them; the pointers to the six DEL-B files become "(deleted; see git <sha>)"); (2) `did_v2_class_provenance.md`, `did_v2_distance_metadata_plan.md`, `did_v2_second_pass_plan.md` and `did_v2_statement_referent_findings.md` move to `docs/history/`; (3) the layout is `docs/` (entry points: tenets, OPEN_WORK, did_query_model), `docs/plans/` (flat), `docs/generated/`, `docs/history/`; (4) the documents are renamed now, in the same move, `V_eta_*` -> `did_v2_*`, every citation updated once; (5) a document cited by bare name resolves to the unique file of that name under `docs/`, and a duplicate name fails the gate. (6) ("A") the seven JSON registers and data files in `schemas/` (`did_v2_carry_register.json`, `did_v2_coverage_ledger.json`, `did_v2_decisions.json`, `did_v2_migration_targets.json`, `did_v2_ndi_ground_truth.json`, `did_v2_openminds_crosswalk.json`, `did_v2_term_registry.json`) are renamed `did_v2_*` in the same step and stay in `schemas/`. Siblings first: DID-matlab and NDI-matlab code that reads a renamed file accepts both names before the old ones go. (DID-matlab `tools/corpus_proven.py`, `tools/affected_corpora.py` and `test-corpus.yml` read `schemas/generated/did_v2_coverage_ledger.json` / `did_v2_corpus_proof.json` by path.) Built: did-schema 8191841 (the move, the rename, the citation rule), 13b3409 (item (6) as amended by [schema folders by family]: the seven registers renamed `did_v2_*`, in `schemas/registry/` and `schemas/generated/`). Built: DID-matlab 4001a598 (#252), d6a52f91 (#317); NDI-matlab ce256e50f (#1077), 1ce002593 (#1109) (both read the `did_v2_*` names, and the old ones, from `schemas/registry/` and `schemas/generated/`; DID-matlab `tools/corpus_proven.py` reads both ledger names).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [pipeline defects are not carries]: ("B", to Claude's question whether carries caused by the migration itself rather than by the v1 data are register rows) a v1 document left unconverted because of the pipeline, not the data, is a DEFECT, never a carry register row: (1) a stale or incomplete V2 schema (DID-matlab `metadata_editor.m`, a form field with no binding; `pyraview.m` / `private/jFrequencyFilter.m`, the pre-[filters are methods] `frequency_filter` path); (2) an NDI second-pass re-fold whose output fails validation and is quarantined (`ndi.migrate.local`: table rows, labels, ensembles); (3) a second-pass step that throws and is caught. Such a run fails loudly, or its report lists the cases as errors apart from carries, and the cause is fixed in code. The carry register explains the data only. Built: DID-matlab 1f83af39 (PR #251) (commits 2495ef9a, 3c717eb8: `+did2/+convert/+migrators_j/metadata_editor.m:378` and `+did2/+convert/+migrators_j/private/jFrequencyFilter.m:254` raise `did2:convert:schemaTooOld`). Built: NDI-matlab 38adc701b (PR #1074) (re-fold quarantines and caught errors reported as errors)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [unresolved recorded elements make a standalone item]: ("1 a") a v1 `stimulus_presentation` whose epoch records elements that resolve to no subject becomes a standalone item, as one with no element at all does (`did_v2_stimulus_model_plan.md` [presentation with no subject]), instead of being carried as v1. The drafted register row carry-082 is withdrawn. Built: NDI-matlab 38adc701b (PR #1074)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [extra field on an ingested stimulus file is malformed]: ("2 a") a v1 `daqmetadatareader_epochdata_ingested` holding a block field or edge the presentation's data body does not write is carried as `malformed` (carry-087): NDI's template block is empty and its writer (`+daq/metadatareader.m` `ingest_epochfiles`) sets only the `daqmetadatareader_id` edge, so such a field is not writer-shaped.

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [NaN control means no control]: ("2 a", after Claude checked the writer) a v1 `control_stimulus_ids` whose control is NaN is converted, not carried: NDI's writer writes NaN when a stimulus set has no control (NDI-matlab main `+app/+stimulus/tuning_response.m:622` "if number of control stimuli is 0, that's okay, just give values of NaN", `:634` `cs_ids = nan(size(stimids))`), and its own reader skips NaN (`:298`). The control-pairing calculation ([control pairing is a calculation]) records NaN for such trials, meaning "no control"; carry-091 keeps only records broken in themselves. Built: NDI-matlab 38adc701b (PR #1074)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [conflicting animal records need a map]: ("yes, cite one correct reading", after asking "why don't we just make the cohort? Is it that we can't tell if the multi-subject is a mistake?") several animals recorded together in a session already get one manipulation on the session's standing cohort (`did_v2_stimulus_model_plan.md` operating rule 2, T17 amendment signed 2026-10-21); only a presentation whose records name DIFFERENT animals (rule 4: "conflicting records are not a shared event") is carried, `needs_mapping`, citing [one correct reading] (carry-081): the generic stage cannot tell which record is right. Built: NDI-matlab 38adc701b (PR #1074).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [method terms are general]: ("1. yes", proposal `docs/v2_working/EMPTY_TERMS_PROPOSAL.md`) a calculation's method term names the general procedure, not the tool or the NDI class that ran it: spike sorting is "Spike sorting" (wikidata:Q2003571), a fit is "curve fitting data transformation" (OBI:0200072); the specific tool (Kilosort, Kiasort, JRCLUST, an `ndi.calc.*` class) is the `software` entity on `software_id` ([software_id is what produced the statement]), carrying its RRID where one exists (Kilosort RRID:SCR_016422). Applies to all seven calculation migrators. Built (did-schema part, the registry rows): did-schema bf6bb62 (wikidata:Q2003571 Spike sorting, OBI:0200072 curve fitting data transformation; an RRID is an identifier on the `software` entity, not a term). Built (in part): DID-matlab 67651bf1 (#282: kilosort and kiasort use wikidata:Q2003571 Spike sorting; contrast_sensitivity_calc uses OBI:0200072 curve fitting). Built: DID-matlab 5236c61 (#306) ([data analysis is the general method], G29: `private/jCalculation.m` and `hartley_calc.m` write EDAM:operation_2945; `jrclust_clusters.m` writes wikidata:Q2003571).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-26 [Naka-Rushton is one function]: ("2. yes") the Naka-Rushton fits are one model term, the function R(c) = Rm c^n / (c^(n s) + c50^(n s)) (NDIcalc-vis-matlab documents RB, RBN and RBNS as this function with different parameters free); RB is written as the constraints n = 1 and s = 1, RBN as s = 1, in the fit's existing constraints field, not as separate terms. Built: empty-ontology b1a12e2 (#32) (EMPTY:0000416, [Naka-Rushton function minted]); did-schema 64a1bf8 (#242) (the registry row); DID-matlab 91d74d0 (#307) (`private/jTuningCurve.m` writes the term with the constraints).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [fields outside the template are malformed]: ("1. Yes", to Claude's proposal: "When a v1 document has a field NDI's template doesn't define, I'd carry the whole document as v1 under one generic \"malformed\" register row instead of dropping the field silently. This comes up in every class family.") a v1 document holding a field, block or edge that its class's NDI template (at the locked `main`) does not define, and that the migrator therefore does not read, is carried whole and unchanged as v1, kind `malformed`, under ONE generic carry-register row for every class (carry-100, `v1_class` "*"); the field is never dropped silently ([lossless means round trip], [partly placed is carried as v1]). Answers "Waiting for Jess" item 4b in `docs/V2_PLAN.md` and the same question for responses (15d), the ingested data cache, images (19c), spike sorting and acquisition systems. Built: did-schema (carry-100). Partly built: DID-matlab e73aec7 (PR #253) (the 72 classes that read raw v1 carry under carry-100, `+did2/+convert/+migrators_j/private/jRawCarry.m:36`). Build pending: DID-matlab (the 23 classes not switched, `rawV1Classes.m`, still take the renamed path, which drops such a field without a note, `jRawCarry.m:14-16`), NDI-matlab (second-pass carries of an off-template field cite carry-100)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [probe location terms]: ("2. Yes", to Claude's proposal: "Probe locations in the corpora use UBERON:0001880, UBERON:0002813 and UBERON:0002812, which aren't in the term registry. They're existing ontology terms, so I'd add them as rows.") the term registry gains three existing UBERON terms, named as UBERON labels them ([term labels stay as the ontology spells them]): "bed nucleus of stria terminalis" (UBERON:0001880; Dab, 404 probe locations, written "bed nucleus of stria terminalis (BNST)"), "right cerebral hemisphere" (UBERON:0002813; 33) and "left cerebral hemisphere" (UBERON:0002812; 29). Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [cell spellings are one meaning]: ("1. Agree", after "Part of the goal is to represent each dataset in the most V2 way possible. Using the term element is not part of the v2 vocabulary", to Claude's proposal: "treat `spikes`, `neuron` and `cell` as three spellings of one meaning, as with [spelling is notation]: the document becomes entity type `cell` and nothing else, and the round trip accepts any of the three back") the v1 element type strings `spikes`, `neuron` and `cell` are not kept as a statement or a note; each becomes entity type `cell`, and the round trip counts the three as equivalent spellings. Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [v1 name is the entity name]: ("I think if v1 has a name write it to entity.name regardless of if it's equal or not", after Claude found that 0 of 1,918 subjects in the 20211116, Dab, JH, Soph and PRED corpora have a `base.name` that differs from `subject.local_identifier`: 45 equal, 1,873 empty) a non-empty v1 `base.name` of a document that becomes an entity is written to `entity.name`, whether or not it equals the local identifier; an empty one writes nothing. Nothing is carried for it. Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [cell spellings amended]: ("1a yes", to Claude's correction: "Today a sorted unit becomes `cell` (single unit), `group` (multi-unit) or is carried (no label), as you decided before. The line I recorded says 'always `cell`'. Reword it to 'the entity type the earlier decisions give, and nothing else kept'?") amends [cell spellings are one meaning]: an element of v1 type `spikes`, `neuron` or `cell` becomes the entity type [sorted units are cells or groups] and [unlabelled units need a map] give it (`cell`, `group`, or carried), not always `cell`; the three spellings remain equivalent in the round trip. Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [element type is a device kind term]: ("Agree", to Claude's proposal after checking the 38 v1 element types: "(i) Drop 'element type' for every element. Each device gets a device-kind term, one per instrument, mapped through a table I'd bring you term by term ... (ii) For patch and sharp, the device is 'patch pipette' or 'sharp electrode'. The mode (voltage, current, attached) goes on the recording observation ... The round trip rebuilds `patch-Vm` from pipette plus voltage. (iii) Until a type has its term, the migrator keeps writing the current statement, so nothing is lost meanwhile") the "element type" assertion holding the v1 word is replaced, per v1 type, by a device-kind term assertion (T1), one term per instrument, each term brought to the team before use; recording mode suffixes (`-Vm`, `-I`, `-Im`, `-attached`) go on the recording observation; the round trip rebuilds the v1 word from kind plus mode; a type with no decided term keeps today's assertion. Built (the device-kind addition): DID-matlab 8552835 (#277: a device whose v1 type has a decided, registered kind gets one Equipment Type (NCIT:C178920) term assertion, `+convert/elementDeviceKind.m`, placed by `+roundtrip/element.m`; the `element type` and `ndi element class` assertions are still written). Found 2026-10-28 (DID-matlab #277): NDI-matlab V2 aa8fbb44b reads the `element type` and `ndi element class` assertions (`+vintage/elementLabel.m`, `elementFields.m`, `objectClass.m`, `isEtaElement.m`, `ndi.session`, `+migrate/+internal/pathSPromotion.m`), so by CLAUDE.md section 3 NDI-matlab's readers change first, before DID-matlab stops writing them. Build pending: NDI-matlab (read the device kind instead of the `element type` assertion, first), did-schema (the per-type term table, `docs/v2_working/ELEMENT_TYPES_DRAFT.md`, for the team); DID-matlab (`+migrators_j/element.m`, `+roundtrip/element.m`).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [ndi element class not kept]: ("1c yes", to Claude's proposal: "Every element also gets 'ndi element class = ndi.neuron' (NDI's software class name). Drop it the same way, with the same check?") the "ndi element class" assertion is not written; the round trip rebuilds `element.ndi_element_class` from the entity type and device kind (for a probe type, NDI's `probetype2object.json`), and an element whose class cannot be rebuilt that way is brought to the team. Build pending: NDI-matlab first (it reads the assertion: `+vintage/elementFields.m`, `objectClass.m`, `isEtaElement.m`, `ndi.session`; found by DID-matlab #277), then DID-matlab (`+migrators_j/element.m`, `+roundtrip/element.m`, with the check).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [site frame is a coordinate system]: ("3 A1", to Claude's proposal: "One coordinate system per probe. Each site's X, Y and Z values link to it. It needs a new optional link on statements, which DID-matlab builds first") probe site positions keep the NCIT X/Y/Z-Coordinate variables ([site positions are coordinates]); each probe gets one `coordinate_system` whose referent is the device entity, with axes x, y, z and NDI's positive directions (left negative / right positive, front negative / back positive; depth's sign and zero point not stated in v1, recorded as unknown), and each site value links to it by a new optional `coordinate_system_id` edge on statements. Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [five probe kinds from NCIT]: ("1. Yes", to Claude's proposal: "Direct hits in NCIT. Accept these five? accelerometer NCIT:C88166 Accelerometer; thermometer NCIT:C50059 Thermometer Device; ppg NCIT:C224112 Photoplethysmography Sensor; optogenetic-fiber NCIT:C50358 Fiber Optic Device; intraoral-cannula NCIT:C50343 Cannula Device ('intraoral' is where it sits)") under [element type is a device kind term], the v1 probe types `accelerometer`, `thermometer`, `ppg`, `optogenetic-fiber` and `intraoral-cannula` take the device kinds NCIT:C88166, NCIT:C50059, NCIT:C224112, NCIT:C50358 and NCIT:C50343 respectively (the cannula's NCIT definition says "introducing medication"; here it delivers a tastant, noted, not a reason to differ). Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [ecg and eeg are recorders]: ("3. Agreed", after "Do you really think electrode device is appropriate for an eeg and ecg?", to Claude's revised proposal: "ECG → electrocardiograph now. EEG keeps today's statement until Wikidata can be checked; if Wikidata has nothing, register `scdo` for it") the v1 probe type `ecg` takes the device kind OBI:0003806 "electrocardiograph"; `eeg` keeps today's assertion until Wikidata is searched for an electroencephalograph, and if Wikidata has none, the `scdo` prefix is registered and SCDO:0000419 "Electroencephalograph" is used. Neither takes NCIT:C49936 "Electrode Device". Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [light-sheet level label is a note]: ("1 It seems like the level number doesn't actually mean anything. Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [ingested stimulus body link follow-up]: ("23. Yes") the ingested stimulus data body, found today by matching description text, gets a proper epoch link as follow-up work, not now: an OPEN_WORK row is filed. Built: did-schema 3a1b316 (OPEN_WORK #158).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [cloud organization id is a global identifier]: ("20. Are we talking about the NDI cloud id? ...", then "20 yes" to Claude's proposal: "give the organization entity the same treatment as the dataset: `global_identifier: ndicloud:<org id>`, with the `ndicloud` prefix widened to cover any NDI Cloud object") a remote dataset's NDI Cloud organization id is written to the organization entity's `global_identifier` as `ndicloud:<org id>`, not to its `name`; `name` is written only from a v1 name; `local_identifier` stays empty. The `ndicloud` prefix's definition widens from "a dataset's id" to "an object's id on NDI Cloud (a dataset or an organization)". The v1 field name is confirmed before the build. Built: did-schema 3a1b316 (`CURIE_lookups_meta.json` `ndicloud` definition). Built (DID-matlab part): DID-matlab c49efa7 (#265).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [curve fitting is the fit method term]: ("24 yes", to Claude's proposal: "Use OBI:0200072 and don't mint \"model fit\"?") the general method term for fitting a function to data is OBI:0200072 "curve fitting"; no EMPTY "model fit" is minted; the function (e.g. Naka-Rushton, Priebe) and its settings are method parameters. Built: did-schema 3a1b316 (term registry row OBI:0200072, registered under OBI's label "curve fitting data transformation").

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [bath solution minted]: ("25 is bath solution actually the right term for the variable? Did you check the wiki data?", then "25 yes" after Claude's search: Wikidata has no "bath solution" or "superfusate"; "artificial cerebrospinal fluid" is Q30314419, a value; "extracellular solution" was not measured; the only ontology hit, REPRODUCE-ME "Bath Solution", has no definition) EMPTY "bath solution" is minted, definition "The solution in which a tissue preparation is immersed or perfused during an experiment.", as the variable [bath is two statements] needs; ACSF as a bath's value is Wikidata Q30314419. Built: empty-ontology 2e5f58e5 (#30, EMPTY:0000409); did-schema (the term registry row, PR #236).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [openMINDS subject leftovers]: ("30 yes. Do we keep track of term synonyms? ...", to Claude's corrected proposal after reading DID-matlab `+migrators_j/openminds_subject.m`, which takes the term from `fields.preferredOntologyIdentifier`) for a v1 openMINDS subject document (one species, strain, genetic strain type or sex about a subject, becoming one term assertion): the term's definition and synonyms are not stored (read back from the term); `matlab_type` is notation; a controlled term's `openminds_id` is rebuilt from the term through the registry's `equivalent` list ([term equivalents recorded]), and a document whose id does not rebuild is carried; a lab-made strain's `openminds_id` goes on the strain entity's `global_identifier` as `openminds:<id>`. Built (DID-matlab part): DID-matlab f4f62e76 (carry-116 and carry-117 sites, `matlab_type` as notation, definition and synonyms not rebuilt). Build pending: NDI-matlab (`+migrate/+internal/subjectStrainAssembly.m` writes the strain entity's `global_identifier` from `openminds_id`, first), then DID-matlab (stop carrying under carry-117).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [term equivalents recorded]: ("equivalent yes. This should also stand for the node that means species, not just the species themselves.") the term registry gains an `equivalent` column: for each term, the ids in other vocabularies that mean the same thing (e.g. openMINDS "Mus musculus" for NCBITaxon:10090; Wikidata ids), including for variable nodes (e.g. the node meaning "species" lists openMINDS's Species type). The openMINDS subject migrator fills it from openMINDS's own mapping. Built: did-schema 3a1b316 (registry column `equivalent`, `check_term_registry` rule R13). Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [dataset form lists carried until homed]: ("31 yes") a v1 dataset form whose subject, species, strain or sex list is non-empty is carried whole as v1 with a needs-home register row until those lists have a V2 home (likely rebuilt from the subjects); revises the earlier drop under [lossless means round trip]. Any curve where that rebuild doesn't match exactly ... is carried.") a tuning curve's `stimid` and `stimulus_presentation_number` are not stored; the round trip rebuilds them from the stimulus presentation and the curve's conditions; a curve that does not rebuild exactly is carried whole as v1. The count over all curves is reported. Built: DID-matlab 05d4dcd (#266)

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [v1 name goes to the class's own name field]: ("42 yes", after Jess: "I thought we planned on removing name from base?" and Claude's check that V2 `base.name` is "did_v1 ONLY (#73 item 54)") a v1 `base.name` is written to the V2 class's own name field where it has one (`entity`, `method`, `acquisition_system`, `element`, `measurement`); for a data body it goes to `notes`; a statement with a non-empty v1 name is carried whole as v1 with a needs-home register row, and the number of such statements is counted in the next corpus run. Where today's decision lines say `description` ([light-sheet level label is a note], [image stack name goes to notes]) the field is `notes`, per [one free-text field: notes]. Built: did-schema 3a1b316 (register row carry-106; OPEN_WORK #156). Built (DID-matlab part, the statements and the count): DID-matlab b185792 (#269: a statement with a non-empty v1 name is carried as v1, carry-106, and counted as `v1_name_statement_carried`).
