Build pending: DID-matlab (the name written to the own `name` field of `method`, `acquisition_system`, `element` and `measurement`, and to a data body's `notes`; until then the name stays in the converted body's `base.name`, which is why V2 `base.name` cannot be deleted yet: DID-matlab #274 CI run 40635518374).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-27 [carried v1 validates against NDI templates]: ("43. Check. It seems to me they should be checking did_v1 templates, not deprecated v2", then "43 yes", after Claude's check that DID-matlab `+did2/+schema/cache.m` reads `deprecated/` and the corpus workflows copy `deprecated/*.json` into the validation set) a document carried whole as v1 is validated against NDI-matlab's v1 templates at the locked NDI `main`, not against did-schema tombstones; then all `schemas/did_v2/deprecated/` tombstones and V2 `base.name` are deleted; NDI-matlab's `base.name` reads that run on V2 documents move to the class's own name field. Order: DID-matlab first, then did-schema, then NDI-matlab. Correction (2026-10-28, Claude, superseding an earlier correction the same day): converted bodies DO carry `base.name`. DID-matlab #274's quick tests, run against the did-schema branch with `base.name` deleted (run 40635518374), failed 37 tests, 31 of them `did2:validation:undeclaredField` 'Property block "base" carries undeclared field "name"' (e.g. `oneepoch`): migrators carry the v1 `base` block forward. `jBase` was never the path. So DID-matlab must first write a v1 name to the class's own field ([v1 name goes to the class's own name field]) and then stop carrying `base.name` on converted bodies; the did-schema deletion (prepared as did-schema 6d1a4d6, reverted until then) waits for that. Found 2026-10-28 (DID-matlab #274 run 40637773135, after empty names were dropped): 33 tests still fail, because v1 documents passed through UNCHANGED are validated against the 73 `retire` classes still in `stable/` and carry `base.name`; resolved by [base.name kept until retire classes leave] (below). Built (DID-matlab part, the template check): DID-matlab ff16053 (#270). Built: DID-matlab 0e6b1b8b (#274: the tombstone tests retired or made to assert both schema states), then did-schema 27da107 (#225: `deprecated/` deleted). Built: NDI-matlab a2938a43 (#1108) (the reads). V2 `base.name` waits, per [base.name kept until retire classes leave], whose own line tracks it.

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [base.name kept until retire classes leave]: ("Whatever you recommend is fine", after "So the end shape is what we decided, you're just asking what order to do things to get there?", to Claude's recommendation (b) on G7, docs/V2_PLAN.md) V2 `base.name` is kept until no v1 document passes through unchanged under a `retire` class in `stable/` (each such class converted or carried as v1); then `base.name` and those classes are deleted together. The 8 `deprecated/` tombstones are deleted now: nothing validates against them once DID-matlab #270's NDI-template check is in. Option (a), stamping an unchanged passthrough `did_v1` inside DID-matlab `v1_to_v2`, is not taken: it would move the stamp [passthrough stays v1: how] put at the end of NDI's pipeline. DID-matlab de810d21 (dropping an empty `base.name` from converted bodies, on #274) is reverted. Built: DID-matlab 0e6b1b8b (#274, de810d21 reverted), did-schema 27da107 (#225, `deprecated/` deleted). Build pending: did-schema (delete `base.name` and the `retire` classes once none passes through).

It's a position mixin on calculation.") a v1 position element's values and its `position_metadata` map to ONE V2 `calculation` with the `position` value-kind mixin, the shape of the Haley V2 import (NDI-matlab `+setup/+conv/+haley/import_V2_decisions.md` #61): `units` becomes the value's unit, `dimensions` the axis terms (e.g. NCIT:C44477, NCIT:C44478), `ontologyNode` the body part in the variable ("<body part> position"); no term observation is written. We don't need Steve to confirm, but we do need this built in order to complete the issue" and "2 include them all") did-schema #66 is built as rescoped on 2026-10-21: a nested quantity field names its value class and the validator expands that class's cell (all value classes, including `count`, `score`, `gain`, `ph`); a derived quantity list; allowed units per [allowed units are closed source_unit lists] for classes with a `source_unit`. A cell with several canonicals (`concentration`) must populate exactly one. #66 is done when the hand-copied cells in `chemical`, `formulation`, `dose` and `frequency_filter` are gone and a wrongly shaped quantity fails validation. Anything beyond that carries") amends [sample times equal within one rounding step]: waveform sample times stored as start + step equal the v1 list when every time differs by at most 8 ulps of the largest absolute time; a list missed by more is carried whole as v1, kind `malformed`. Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-28 [device kinds minted]: ("D1 yes", to Claude's proposal after Wikidata (via its SPARQL service, 2026-10-28) and OLS4 (2026-10-27) had no item for them: "mint EMPTY terms for these six now, each with a one-line definition brought for approval; add a Wikidata id later as `equivalent` if one appears") EMPTY terms are minted for patch pipette, sharp electrode, electrical stimulator, lick spout and reward well (device kinds of the v1 element types `patch*`, `sharp*`, `stimulator`, `lick-spout`, `reward-well`) and for the cell-attached recording mode (on the recording observation, [element type is a device kind term]); the labels, definitions and synonyms are approved in [device kind labels and synonyms]. Built: empty-ontology 1e489c23 (#31, EMPTY:0000411-0000415); did-schema (the term registry rows, PR #236). Built: DID-matlab a87114fd (#304) (`elementDeviceKind.m` writes EMPTY:0000411-0000415; cell-attached is OBI:0002179, [cell-attached is OBI]).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-28 [device kind labels and synonyms]: ("D1. Does it make sense to say patch pipette but sharp electrode? Reward well is a little funny sounding.", "Can the others be saved as synonyms ...?", then "Approve D1. Probably should add intracellular electrode") refines [device kinds minted]: the six EMPTY terms are labelled and defined as follows, with synonyms per empty-ontology `docs/conventions.md` section 4: patch electrode ("A glass micropipette whose tip is sealed against a cell membrane to record or control the electrical activity of the cell or of a membrane patch."; exact: patch pipette, patch-clamp electrode); sharp electrode ("A fine-tipped glass micropipette electrode that penetrates the cell membrane to record intracellular electrical activity."; exact: sharp microelectrode; related: intracellular electrode, intracellular microelectrode, since a whole-cell patch electrode also records intracellularly); electrical stimulator ("A device that delivers electrical current or voltage pulses to tissue to evoke a response."; exact: stimulator); lick spout ("A drinking spout that delivers liquid to an animal and detects its licks."; exact: lickometer spout; related: lick port); reward port ("A site in a behavioural apparatus at which an animal receives a food or liquid reward."; exact: reward dispenser; related: reward well); cell-attached recording, a recording mode, not a device kind ("A patch-clamp configuration in which the pipette is sealed to the intact cell membrane without rupturing it."; exact: cell-attached patch, on-cell recording). Built: empty-ontology 1e489c2 (#31) (the five device kinds with these labels and synonyms); did-schema dd495e6 (#236) (registry rows); DID-matlab a87114fd (#304) (`elementDeviceKind.m`). Cell-attached recording is not minted: [cell-attached is OBI].

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-28 [tuning best value is a condition]: ("E1 explain. Does it deserve a V2 home?", then "E1 yes" to Claude's proposal: the angle a v1 tuning curve's `angle ... best` selection chose, stored only in its `log` ("angle best value is 90."), becomes a condition on the calculation (e.g. orientation = 90 degrees, true of every point of the curve), and the rule becomes a method parameter (e.g. orientation selection = best); the migration reads the value from the log once, and the stimid rebuild ([tuning stimids rebuild]) works from the conditions) a curve whose log does not state the chosen value is carried as before (carry-056). Built: DID-matlab f6bff58 (#272: `did2.convert.tuningBestValues` reads the chosen value from the log; condition and `<variable> selection = best` method parameter; the stimid rebuild uses the condition; 20211116: 84 of 84 tuning curves convert, was 20).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-28 [one calculation per named calculator output]: ("Yes", to Claude's proposal after checking T10, which already records Lepsky et al.'s "one output document type" and migration "1->1 with base.id and depends_on preserved", "And just to be clear not all calculations are like this. Just the ones that come from v1 named calcualtors") for the v1 named calculators only (the NDIcalc-vis tuning calculators -- orientation/direction, contrast, spatial frequency, temporal frequency, speed -- and the other named v1 calculators), one v1 calculator output becomes ONE V2 calculation, 1->1 and id-preserving (T10); its several fits are entries of the value's `model_fit` array, each naming its own `model` with fixed parameters in `constraints`; the method holds no `function` list. So a spatial- or temporal-frequency output's five fits (difference of Gaussians, Movshon, Movshon with C, spline, Gaussian of log frequency) are five `model_fit` entries, and a speed output's three Priebe fits are three entries with xi free, held at 0 and held at 1 in `constraints` (Priebe et al. 2006: xi = 0, preferred temporal frequency independent of spatial frequency; xi = 1, fully speed tuned). Calculations that do not come from a named v1 calculator are not bound by this. Amends [tuning fits use curve fitting now] (no `function` list) and answers E2 and E3. T10 is amended to say so. Built: DID-matlab f6bff58 (#272: no `function` list on the method; speed's three Priebe entries already held xi free / 0 / 1). The `abs` fits wait on [absolute-value fits are fit entries] (G5).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-28 [absolute-value fits are fit entries]: ("Let's revisit 36a with E2-3", then "Yes") supersedes [absolute-value fits are a second calculation]: a spatial- or temporal-frequency output's `abs` fits are `model_fit` entries of the same calculation, each marked with a new optional fit-entry field `response_transform` ("absolute value"), not a second calculation with a minted id, which conflicted with T10. If the v1 `abs` block turns out to hold more than the same fits on |response| (e.g. its own curve), Claude brings that back before building. Built (did-schema part): did-schema f02a251 (`response_transform` on the `model_fit` entry). Built: DID-matlab dee59f88 (PR #313) (the SF/TF migrators and round trips; the second calculation is removed), after [interpolated values are a list] answered the hold (G5). It had been HELD (2026-10-28, per this line's own condition): DID-matlab #272 found the v1 `abs` block also holds `fitless`, the interpolated values of |response| (all 5,604 Soph SF/TF calculator documents; 1,987 differ from the main `fitless`); the question is G5 in docs/V2_PLAN.md.

Narrows [epoch-less presentations carry] to presentations that name an epoch.

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [linked session link on a direct dataset relation]: ("a. If a session is in a dataset and a session is in a study, can it be inferred that the study is in the dataset?", Claude's answer: not from those two facts; NDI's V2 writer files each study `included_in` the dataset explicitly, `ndi.setup.V2.datasetMetadata`) a linked session's linking method ("database linking" and its Pathname) lives on one direct session -> dataset `included_in` relation, always written; the study relations stay as they are. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G3. Built: NDI-matlab 15172b123 (`ndi.setup.V2.writeDataset`, `datasetSessions`, `datasetDatabaseFiles`, `ndi.dataset` convert and unlink; #1103).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [fit model stated once]: ("wouldn't that be the only class that isn't required to state its method?", "Would removing it make query harder?", then "Yes" to Claude's answers: the method keeps its term OBI:0200072 and other parameters; each fit entry's queryable `model` finds every fit, the method parameter only single-model ones) a calculation whose fits share one model writes no `{function: <model>}` method parameter; the model is stated in each fit entry. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G6. Built: DID-matlab 22cb51d2 (#289, `private/jCalculation.m` `fitMethod`; the round trip needed no change).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [term names compare by node]: ("So the recommendation is do nothing and allowing the name difference because the meaning is the same? If so, I agree") a v1 term name that differs from the registry label of the same node (e.g. v1 "bed nucleus of stria terminalis (BNST)", UBERON:0001880) is notation: no change, and the round trip compares terms by node. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G12. Built: DID-matlab 22cb51d2 (#289, `+roundtrip/private/nameByNode.m`).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [species text is the openMINDS equivalent]: ("It seems like we shouldn't add info on a registry row? Do we need this info? Are there other existing terms that give a definition and synonyms for c elegans", then "Agreed" to Claude's revision: NCBITaxon:6239 has no definition and only the synonym "Rhabditis elegans" (OLS4, 2026-10-29); openMINDS's species instance `openminds.om-i.org/instances/species/caenorhabditisElegans` has a definition and the synonym "C. elegans", with NCBITaxon_6239 as its preferred identifier) a v1 openMINDS subject's species `definition` and `synonym` are not stored in V2: the term registry records the openMINDS species instance as the term's equivalent ([term equivalents recorded]), and the round trip reports the v1 text as an accepted difference. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G10. Built: did-schema ffc82c8 (NCBITaxon:6239's `equivalent`); DID-matlab 73899897 (#293, `+roundtrip/openminds_subject.m`).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [openMINDS element leftovers carried]: ("Explain a bit more", then "Fine.") a v1 `openminds_element` holding an `openminds_id` or a `fields.description` is carried whole as v1, as for openMINDS subjects ([openMINDS subject leftovers]). Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G11. Built: did-schema ffc82c8 (carry-128); DID-matlab 73899897 (#293, `+migrators_j/openminds_element.m`).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [segment length is the time chunk]: ("17. Show me. Do we really think this needs to be fully round tripped?", "What would that look like in v2? Is this how new v2 datasets would look?", "Do you think this is genuinely the right way to represent this type of information in v2?", "So no new schema change, just migration change?", then "Yes.") an NDI segment length (v1 `daqreader_mfdaq_epochdata_ingested.parameters.sample_analog_segment` / `sample_digital_segment`, which NDI's reader uses to find a sample's segment file, `+ndi/+daq/+reader/mfdaq.m:245-253`) is the time dimension's `chunk` on the data body (`data.dimensions[k].chunk`, `sampled_body` only), format EMPTY:0000367 "NDI binary format"; no schema change. The v1 archive document is not kept: its id goes under [a rebuilt document may lose its id] unless something links to it; the `ingestion_manifest` stays the ingestion record. NDI V2's ingest and reader move first (they write and read the v1 document today, `mfdaq.m:778-784`); NDI's second pass fills `chunk` when it re-describes the segment files. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G9. Build pending: NDI-matlab (ingest writes `chunk`; the reader reads it; the second pass fills it from the v1 document), DID-matlab (the archive document is not kept).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [one folder, final classes only]: ("We want to move towards all v2 schema files being at the same folder level. All tombstones and deprecated removed. No draft or in progress", "By retire you mean delete the json files?", then "Ok. Yes. Your description matches what the folder should look like") every V2 class lives in one folder, `schemas/did_v2/`, with no `stable/` / `draft/` tiers and no `maturity_level`; no class is `in_progress`; no `retire` class remains: a retire class's JSON file is deleted (its name added to `tools/veta_record.py` RETIRED_CLASSES) once no v1 document passes through the generic migration under it, a document still carried as v1 being checked against NDI's own v1 template. The siblings that read the tiers by path (DID-matlab and NDI-matlab workflows and tools) move to the flat layout first. Partly built: NDI-matlab 1ce00259 (#1109) (reads the flat and family layouts); did-schema 13b3409 (no `stable/` / `draft/` tiers: the draft and persist classes are in `schemas/did_v2/` by family, as [schema folders by family] amends "one folder"). Partly built: did-schema 4be8855 (no `maturity_level`), 0273b41 (no class is `in_progress`; validate_v_eta refuses it), c648f65 (four more retire classes deleted, [retire check now]). Build pending: did-schema (the 21 retire classes that remain: each deleted when nothing passes through it), DID-matlab and NDI-matlab (read the flat layout first; stop passing documents through under retire classes).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [v1 writes into a V2 session]: ("I feel like they should be writing v2 documents, but I'm open to temporary solutions that move our work thru faster. But ultimately, I think all writers need to be writing v2 before we can say migration is complete.") for now, a v1-template document NDI writes into a session with a V2 database is stamped did_v1 and checked against NDI's own v1 template, as a carried document is, so no retire file is needed for it; the end state is that every NDI writer writes V2 documents and a V2 session accepts only V2 documents, and the migration is not complete until then. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G46. Build pending: NDI-matlab (did2sqlite stamps and checks a v1 write; then each v1 writer moves to V2), did-schema (the retire files of the 18 classes deleted once nothing passes through them).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [linked session link details]: ("Yes to 1-3.") on the direct session -> dataset `included_in` relation of [linked session link on a direct dataset relation], as NDI writes it: (1) an ingested session gets the same relation, with method "database ingestion" (EMPTY:0000399); (2) converting a session between linked and ingested replaces that relation (a new one with the new method), not edits it; (3) its method carries the Pathname and "software class" (`ndi.session.dir`), as the DID-matlab migrator writes when `session_creator` is stated. Relative vs absolute Pathname and the scope of `ndi.dataset.addSessionV2` are still open (docs/V2_PLAN.md G40 (4), (5)). Built: NDI-matlab 915bbb3 (`add_linked_session`, `add_ingested_session` and the two convert functions write and replace the relation; #1103).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [reshaped retire classes carried]: ("B") v1 `distance_metadata` and `oneepoch` are carried whole as v1 for now, not reshaped into their retire classes; the reshape waits for their V2 homes (`oneepoch`: docs/V2_PLAN.md G44; `distance_metadata`: a decision still to make). Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G50. Built: did-schema 4e68a6cc (carry-147, carry-148); DID-matlab 42d454e4 (#296, both carried whole as v1). Built: did-schema 3026c1e (the two retire files deleted, #242).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [linked session pathname]: ("Okay. I agree with those proposals.", to Claude's proposal: keep relative inside the dataset folder, absolute outside) the Pathname on the session -> dataset relation of [linked session link details] is relative to the dataset's folder (written with '/') when the session folder is inside it, absolute otherwise, as `ndi.v2.linkPath` writes today. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G40 (4). Built: NDI-matlab 915bbb3 (the relation's method parameter written with `ndi.v2.linkPath`; #1103).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [addSessionV2 folded back]: ("Okay. I agree with those proposals.", to Claude's proposal) `ndi.dataset.addSessionV2`, the helper `add_linked_session` and `add_ingested_session` share, is in scope for [linked session link details]; once no v1 branch remains ([v1 writes into a V2 session]) it is folded back into those two functions or renamed without the V2 suffix. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G40 (5). Built: NDI-matlab 915bbb3 (the relation written by `addSessionV2`; #1103). Build pending: NDI-matlab (the fold or rename after the v1 branches go).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [term names match ignoring spacing]: ("Okay. I agree with those proposals.", to Claude's proposal (a)) a registry term is found by its name ignoring case, spaces, underscores and hyphens, as relation and entity-type handles already are (DID-matlab `+did2/+build/vocabulary.m` `normalize`), so "path name" finds Pathname; no two registered names may collide under that rule. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G56. Built (did-schema): `tools/check_term_registry.py` R2 compares names that way (PR #236). Built: DID-matlab aa72d21b (#294, `did2.schema.termNameKey` keys `termIndex.byName`, with a test).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [n-trode count from the channel list]: ("Ok", to Claude's proposal) the "number of electrodes" of an n-trode ([n-trode is a multielectrode array], [number of electrodes minted]) is the count of channels its epochprobemap `devicestring` names (NDI `+ndi/+epoch/epochprobemap_daqsystem.m`, "the device and channels that comprise the data"), cross-checked against the sites of its `probe_geometry` / `site2channelmap` where one exists; when they disagree the device is carried as v1 under a `needs_mapping` register row. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G14. Built (did-schema part): carry-156 (needs_mapping). Built: DID-matlab a78299d8 (#325) (the count from the epochprobemap, the cross-check, citing carry-156).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [imaging mode is a method term]: ("Yes to both") one- vs two-photon imaging, whose devices both take the kind "microscope", is kept as a method term on the recording observation, as the patch and sharp recording modes are. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G15. Build pending: DID-matlab (the element migrator writes the mode as the method term), did-schema (the mode terms in the registry).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [equipment type is the device kind list]: ("Yes to both") the NCIT:C178920 "Equipment Type" binding becomes a closed list of the registered device kinds instead of the OBI:0000968 root. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G17. Built: did-schema (the binding's `values`: the 16 registered device kinds, the OBI:0000968 root removed).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [V2 ingest is one sampled body per channel group]: ("Yes to both") NDI V2's ingest writes one `sampled_body` per channel group, owned by the epoch's recording observation; its time dimension carries the registry's time term and the segment length as its `chunk` ([segment length is the time chunk]); a reader finds an epoch's data through those bodies, not through the v1 `daqreader_mfdaq_epochdata_ingested` document. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G48. Build pending: NDI-matlab (ingest writes the bodies; `+ndi/+daq/reader.m` reads them).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [short last chunk]: ("Yes to both") a body's last chunk may be short: it holds what remains. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G47. Built: did-schema (the `chunk` documentation). DID-matlab needs no build: it has no check of chunk sizes (a read-only triage of DID-matlab V2 80a94a05, 2026-10-30, found none).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [missing facts are needs info]: ("Ii", to Claude's option (ii), after "Do we not have an option for needs info?") a document may record a fact its source never stated as a `needs_info` entry naming the missing field and why, instead of a placeholder value or a carry: a required field may be absent only when the document's `needs_info` names it; the migration report counts the entries; a dataset's import may fill the field and drop the entry; the field stays required for every other writer. First use: a probe's `coordinate_system`, whose `reference_point` and depth `positive_direction` v1's `probe_geometry` never states (its template has no field for either), so the site positions migrate to V2 ([site frame is a coordinate system]). Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G37 (the z-axis and left/right, front/back terms are still to be looked up and registered). Built: DID-matlab 01765b76 (#295, `did2.build` 'NeedsInfo', the validator and `did2.schema.needsInfoFields`); did-schema (the optional `base.needs_info` field; `coordinate_system` documents it). Built: DID-matlab 24275dba (`+migrators_j/probe_geometry.m` writes the entries; `reportNote` counts them).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [vintage-B ontologyImage cases]: ("yes to both") (a) a v1 `ontologyImage` with no non-empty edge at all (NDI `imageDocMaker.m` makes one when given no row) is carried whole as v1 like the row-only case (carry-124); (b) one with a subject or element edge beside its row (no NDI writer makes one) is carried as `malformed`. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G43 (did-schema #92). Built (did-schema part): carry-124 widened; carry-150 (malformed). Built: DID-matlab 34deec0 (#305, `+migrators_j/ontology_image.m` cites them).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [element name and reference stored apart]: ("a") a migrated `element` writes its v1 name, exactly, to the entity's `name`, and keeps `"<name> (ref <N>)"` in `local_identifier` as the dataset-unique handle; the reference is read back as what follows the known name, so every name and reference rebuild, including a name that contains " (ref ". No new term and no schema change; carry-055 is no longer needed. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G55 (did-schema #149, OPEN_WORK #142). Built: DID-matlab 0e25c5b (`+migrators_j/element.m` writes `name`; its round trip reads the reference after the name; no carry-055 site left; #299), NDI-matlab 461d8a6 (`+vintage/elementFields.m` reads the name from `entity.name`), did-schema (carry-055 retired).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-29 [unit with no probe in the batch carried]: ("yes") a sorted unit whose probe is not in the migration batch, so its subject cannot be rebuilt from the probe ([unit direct and subject rebuild from the probe]), is carried whole as v1 under a register row instead of converting with its subject unchecked. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G16. Built (did-schema part): carry-151. Built: DID-matlab 34deec0 (#305, the unit migrator's `unit_probe_not_in_batch` case carries).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [table rows carried as v1]: ("B", after "An ontology table row is a fairly poorly defined class for v2 conversion. There is no way to know without reading the semantic meaning whether each column refers to a v1 subject or an undefined entity like the patches or plates in Haley dataset") the generic migration carries every v1 `ontologyTableRow` whole as v1; it resolves no subject and fans out no statements, because a row does not state which entity each column is about. Each dataset's import maps its own tables ([migration not generalizable]). Supersedes the generic subject resolution of NDI's second pass (`+migrate/+internal/ontologyRowSubjects.m`) and makes [old table rows counted and carried] / [old table rows carried by NDI] moot (every row is carried, pre-repair or not; docs/V2_PLAN.md G45 moot). Built (did-schema part): carry-026's `when` widened to every row. Built: DID-matlab 888f1415 (`+migrators_j/ontology_table_row.m` carries every row, citing carry-026; the encounter map left the generic stage), NDI-matlab 8a193422f (the second pass stops resolving table-row subjects), did-schema d906767 (the `ontology_table_row` retire file deleted) and the fold of carry-098 and carry-125 into carry-026 (`git grep` on both siblings' V2 finds no site citing either).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [preset name stays on method]: ("Ok. I'll go with your recommendation", after asking whether a preset should be a text parameter or a child class of `method`) a saved parameter set's name (NDI `spikeextractor` / `spikesorter` look one up by name, e.g. "default") stays the optional `name` field of the standalone `method` document. It is not a parameter: a parameter is a setting, and a name changes nothing about how the method runs (T16). It is not a child class: a preset is not a different kind of method, only a method saved and named for reuse (T1, T12; [method]). Confirms the method plan's "`method.name` (the protocol name, kept)". No build: the schema already holds it.

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [redeclaration merge moot]: ("G41 can be moot") no merge of a redeclared field into its parent is built. In the final V2 shape no field is redeclared: every redeclaration today is in a `retire` class (`element`, `measurement`, `stimulus_parameter`, `probe_location`, `pyraview`, `daqreader_image_epochdata_ingested`) or is a class's own `name` over the did_v1-only `base.name` (`entity`, `method`, `acquisition_system`), which stops being a redeclaration when `base.name` is deleted (tools/check_constraint_refinement.py, 2026-10-30: 8 field and 1 edge redeclarations). Supersedes the DID-matlab build of [redeclarations merge when base.name goes]; [redeclarations only narrow] stays enforced by `check_constraint_refinement.py --enforce`. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G41. did-schema #99 closes when `base.name` is deleted.

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [element name kept apart, base.name carried]: ("I", to Claude's option (i), after Claude corrected the premise of [element name and reference stored apart]: the `local_identifier` has been NDI's `<name> | <reference>` since [element local identifier is name and reference], not `name (ref N)`) [element name and reference stored apart] stands with `local_identifier` as `<name> | <reference>`: a migrated element writes its v1 `element.name` exactly to `entity.name`, and its reference is read back as what follows the known name, so the cases carried today under carry-055 (an empty name with a reference, a non-integer or text reference, a name that reads as a pair) convert. An element whose v1 `base.name` is non-empty (NDI's `ndi.element/newdocument` never writes one) is carried whole as v1 as `malformed`, instead of its `base.name` going to `entity.name` ([v1 name is the entity name] no longer applies to elements). Built (did-schema part): carry-149 (malformed). Built: DID-matlab 0e25c5b (#299, cites carry-149); did-schema (carry-055 retired: `git grep carry-055 origin/V2` finds no site in DID-matlab or NDI-matlab); NDI-matlab 461d8a6 (`+vintage/elementFields.m` reads `entity.name` first).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [Naka-Rushton function minted]: ("Yes") a new EMPTY term, label "Naka-Rushton function", definition "A saturating function of contrast, R(c) = Rm c^n / (c^(n s) + c50^(n s)), used to describe the contrast response of a neuron.", exact synonym "Naka-Rushton equation", under NCIT:C16870 Mathematical Model; it is the model term of [Naka-Rushton is one function] (OLS4, 2026-10-28: no existing term; Wikidata Q52425525 is an article). Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G8. Built: empty-ontology b1a12e2 (mint; #32); did-schema (the registry row). Built: DID-matlab 91d74d0 (`jTuningCurve.m` oneFunction writes the term; the round trip reads the node and the old bare label; #307).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [synonyms supersede nodes only]: ("Yes to g39") [spelling is notation] (2026-10-26), which puts `synonyms` on term registry rows (built in did-schema ccd05db), supersedes [registry holds nodes] (2026-10-22) where that said the registry carries no synonym field. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G39. No build.

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [interpolated values are a list]: ("Okay", to Claude's proposal after being shown NDIcalc-vis-matlab `+ndi/+calc/+vis/spatial_frequency_tuning.m` lines 279-300, which analyse the curve and |response| and store `abs.fitless` beside the five abs fits) a tuning calculation's `interpolated_values` becomes a list of entries like `model_fit`, each with the optional `response_transform` ("absolute value" on the one read from |response|), so one calculation holds both, as T10 requires; extends [absolute-value fits are fit entries]. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G5. Built: DID-matlab 5236c61 (#306: the tuning folds write a list, the abs `fitless` as its second entry, once the loaded schema declares it; the round trip reads either shape); NDI-matlab needs nothing (`git grep interpolated_values origin/V2 -- src` finds no reader at 6408f46). Built: DID-matlab 51b4c7a (#310: the tests read either shape, and run both on schema copies), did-schema (`interpolated_values` a list on the spatial-frequency, temporal-frequency and contrast tuning calculations and `contrast_sensitivity`, each entry with `response_transform`).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [canonical absent only as needs info]: ("Okay", to Claude's proposal after "Why would the source unit have no conversion? ... So if we know that an ingredient was added, but not how much, we state the ingredient but give it no value?") every quantity holds exactly one canonical value; it may be absent only when the document's `needs_info` names it ([missing facts are needs info]): a source number with no unit (v1 treatment amounts, DID-matlab `jDoseBodies.m`), or an amount the source never states, which is left out (the ingredient is still listed) rather than written as `source_value` NaN (T14: missing is not a value). A unit spelling the migrator does not recognise is carried for mapping, not written without a canonical. Decibels are not an exception: a dB quantity is a `gain` value whose canonical field is `decibels` itself ([decibels are their own canonical]). Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G38. Built (did-schema part): carry-155 (an unrecognised unit spelling). Built in part: DID-matlab 34deec0 (#305: `jDoseBodies.m` names a missing ingredient amount, and a number with no unit its canonical, in needs_info; `probe_geometry`'s unknown unit carries citing carry-155). Partly built: DID-matlab 1ae72adc (#318) (the validator requires a quantity's canonical unless needs_info names it). Build pending: DID-matlab (the validator's quantity-shape check requires the canonical unless needs_info names it; the all-blank formulation, docs/V2_PLAN.md G67; `jDoseValue`'s `volume.liters = NaN`). Built (did-schema part): the quantity documentation (each value class's canonical field says it is absent only when `base.needs_info` names it). Built: DID-matlab 80a94a0 (#311: the unit table's Molar / molar / M rows give a concentration its `molar` canonical; a concentration unit the table does not know carries, carry-155; prefixed molar units wait on their registry rows).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [trigger channels named by their link]: ("Ok") a channel-based sync rule's two trigger channels are the clock alignment configuration's method parameters labelled "from trigger channel" and "to trigger channel", matching its `from_acquisition_system_id` / `to_acquisition_system_id` links, instead of the v1 keys `daqsystem ch1` / `daqsystem ch2`. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G13. Built: DID-matlab 91d74d0 (`+migrators_j/syncrule.m` labels; its round trip reads both; #307), NDI-matlab 307f7a6 (`syncruleParameters` reads the new labels, and the old ones for sessions already migrated; #1101).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [decibels are their own canonical]: ("Show me. I think a", after being shown did-schema `stable/gain.json` and DID-matlab `jFrequencyFilter.m`) a quantity stated in decibels is a `gain` value whose canonical field is `decibels`, with no conversion to a linear ratio (which would need the power-or-amplitude kind and, for dB SPL or dBm, a reference level the source may not state). This is the shape already built; DID-matlab `jSourceUnit.m` lists dB with no factor only because its general table converts other units into their canonical one. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G57. No build.

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [Haley mask words leave the generic stage]: ("Yes") the image-mask region words of DID-matlab `+migrators_j/image_stack.m` ("arena" -> arena region, "bacteria"/"lawn" -> bacterial lawn region, "reference mark"; docs/v2_working/DATASET_SPECIFIC_AUDIT.md D5) move to the Haley V2 import's map; the generic stage carries a mask whose region it cannot read. The encounter map (audit Q1) already left the generic stage (DID-matlab #250), and table rows are carried ([table rows carried as v1]). Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G52. Built (did-schema part): carry-154. Built: DID-matlab 34deec0 (#305, `image_stack.m` keeps mask-vs-map detection, the region words gone since #250, an unread region carried citing carry-154). Built: NDI-matlab 011c97d16 (#1021) (the Haley V2 import's `+setup/+conv/+haley/geometryDocuments.m` writes the arena, reference mark and bacterial lawn masks; it predates the line).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [control records carried in pass 1]: ("Yes to both") (G18) a v1 `control_stimulus_ids` record is carried whole as v1 in DID's pass 1 with a note, under its own register row of kind `not_modelled` with `stimulus_presentation`'s reason (carry-045: decomposed by NDI's second pass, [second pass reads control stimuli from v1]); (G34) an `app` field outside the control template is malformed (carry-100), as NDI-matlab #1093 built. Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [image raw round trip and single-read keys]: ("Yes to both") (G42) a raw-v1 round trip is built for `image` when `image` moves to the raw-v1 path, carrying the label back and rebuilding `format` 'tiff' from EDAM:format_3591; (G36) a second value of a key the grating recipe reads once stays carried (carry-080), with no home designed until a dataset holds one (0 cases in B, Dab and 20211116). Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G42, G36. Built: DID-matlab 9edf8991 (#315) (`+roundtrip/image.m`, added in 8e54ffc1, rebuilds `format` 'tiff' from EDAM:format_3591; `image` reads raw v1).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [age is NCIT Age]: ("Ok") the variable of the age-at-injection observation ([age at injection is an observation]) is NCIT:C25150 "Age" ("How long something has existed; elapsed time since birth."), the most general term whose zero point is birth, as postnatal day counts from; "at injection" is said by the observation's time reference, not the term (T19). Chosen over PATO:0000011 and EFO:0000246 "age" (no zero point named) and NCIT:C172691 (too specific). Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G51. Built (did-schema part): the registry row. Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [equivalents filled in did-schema]: ("B") the term registry's `equivalent` column ([term equivalents recorded]) is filled by a one-time did-schema data change, made by a did-schema script and reviewed in its PR, not by a DID-matlab tool: the registry lives in did-schema and DID-matlab only reads it. The first fill is the species rows from openMINDS's species instances. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G33. Built: did-schema f461112 (`tools/fill_term_equivalents.py`, exact equivalents only; the species fill adds no id: of openMINDS's 29 species instances only caenorhabditisElegans names a registered node, NCBITaxon:6239, filled in ffc82c8).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [Hartley settings on the inline method]: ("Ok") a Hartley presentation's generator settings that the shared Hartley basis does not read (`randState`, `rect`, `distance`, `chromhigh`, `reps`, ...) are parameters of the presentation manipulation's inline `method`, beside its shared `method_id` to the basis, as [method shared plus own] (2026-10-22) allows. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G35. Built: NDI-matlab 6408f46 (the second pass's stimulus decomposition, `hartleyBasisGratings` and the presentation's inline method; #1102). DID-matlab needs nothing: no file at its V2 3c6c4d70 names carry-080 (`git grep -c carry-080 origin/V2` finds none).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [acquisition time on its clock]: ("G28 ok") a v1 image stack's acquisition time (a MATLAB datenum on a named clock, written today as the condition "acquisition timestamp (<clock>)", DID-matlab `image_stack.m`) leaves the label: on a UTC clock it is the observation's time reference on that clock, the value converted to seconds; on any other clock the datenum cannot be converted without assuming UTC, so `source_value` is kept, the canonical is left out and named in `needs_info` ([missing facts are needs info]). The variable is AFO:AFR_0001158 "acquisition time" ([afo prefix registered]). Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G28. Built: DID-matlab 5236c61 (`image_stack.m`, `+roundtrip/imageStack.m`; #306; the needs_info condition is accepted by `checkRules` conditions_one_value when needs_info names its canonical). Open: docs/V2_PLAN.md G65 (relative vs absolute reference on a UTC clock).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [data analysis is the general method]: ("G29 ok") the method term of a calculation that is not a fit (a tuning curve without a fit, `hartley_calc`, `stimulus_response_scalar`) is EDAM:operation_2945 "Data analysis" ("Apply analytical methods to existing data of a specific type"), per [method terms are general]; JRCLUST takes wikidata:Q2003571 "Spike sorting" and the generic fits OBI:0200072 "curve fitting data transformation", as kilosort and contrast_sensitivity_calc already do. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G29. Built (did-schema part): EDAM:operation_2945's registry row. Built: DID-matlab 5236c61 (the calculation migrators; #306). Open: docs/V2_PLAN.md G66 ([hartley method implied] names the replaced term).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [image collection carried until a writer]: ("G24 ok. Tho note, this will probably be just an intensity observation with multiple data bodies") NDI has an `imageCollection` template but no writer of `imageCollection_id` at the locked NDI main (only the template and schema files name it), so a v1 image with a non-empty `imageCollection_id` is carried whole as v1, `malformed` (input no writer produces), and no group is designed. Jess's note for when a writer or dataset uses it: a collection is probably one intensity observation with several data bodies, not a group. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G24; narrows [image file details placed]'s `member_of` part. Built (did-schema part): carry-153 (malformed). Built: DID-matlab 34deec0 (#305, `image.m`, `image_stack.m` cite it; no DID-matlab file cites carry-013 any more, so did-schema retired it).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [global-clock oneepoch details]: ("G44 ok") when the global-clock `oneepoch` calculation is built (carried as v1 meanwhile, carry-148): (1) its `input_id` links to the per-epoch recordings are made by NDI's second pass, which mints them where pass 1 has none; (2) its `variable` comes from the element type (`derivedSeriesKind`); (3) its data stays an `opaque_body`, re-described by NDI; (4) `redundant: true` goes on the body; (5) a `whole_session_<ref>` one, for which fork A1 mints no epoch, is anchored to the session. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G44 (did-schema #102). Build pending: NDI-matlab (the second pass mints and links), DID-matlab (the calculation; carry-148 retired), did-schema (carry-148 retired).

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-10-30 [openMINDS strain leftovers settled]: ("G49 ok", after Claude corrected two claims) for [openMINDS strain leftovers]: the `breedingType` assertion's variable is NCIT:C42877 "Breeding" (no term is minted; its value is the openMINDS breeding-type term, or NCIT:C28422 "Inbred Strain" / EFO:0004430 "outbred"); the `diseaseModel` assertion's variable is BAO:0010279 "disease model"; a `stockNumber` becomes an RRID when its vendor is JAX (`RRID:IMSR_JAX:<n>`), MMRRC (`RRID:MMRRC_<id>`) or Bloomington (`RRID:BDSC_<n>`), each form resolved at scicrunch.org/resolver on 2026-10-30 (IMSR_JAX:000664 C57BL/6J; MMRRC_000231-UNC; BDSC_3605 w[1118]); any other vendor, CGC included (a WormBase RRID needs the WBStrain id, `RRID:WB-STRAIN:WBStrain…`, which a CGC stock number does not give), keeps the Catalog Number (NCIT:C99286) text assertion; the vendor is read from the linked openMINDS Organization's short name; several identifiers all go in `global_identifier` (a list); `lookupLabel` is openMINDS's generated display label, not stored and regenerated by the round trip. Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G49 (did-schema #158). Built (did-schema part): registry rows NCIT:C42877, BAO:0010279 (BAO was already registered). Answers docs/V2_PLAN.md G69 (Q1, Q2). Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-11-01 [CLAUDE.md layout corrections drafted]: ("4. Yes", to Claude's question "may I draft the update [CLAUDE.md section 3 still says a class can be in_progress (removed 10-31) and its folder list is out of date (no relation/, acquisition/, docs/references/)] for your review in the next PR? Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-11-01 [migration record]: ("Yes", to Claude's question "a new standalone class `migration` (not a statement), one per run, with the method, the report as its body, and `base.needs_info` dropped?", after "I didn’t think you were going to store it as a field on base. That seems bizarre", "I don’t understand why it has to be saved in the document itself?", "I’m not loving either option. I want v2 schema to feel clean and these suggestions feel cluttered somehow", "Should the report be a document in v2? That way it stays with the dataset?" and "Should it be a calculation? Or a new class called migration?") replaces where [missing facts are needs info] records a fact the source never stated: `base.needs_info` is dropped, so a data document no longer says what its source left out; a new standalone class `migration`, not a statement, is written once per migration run; its method is the term for format conversion plus parameters (the tool, DID-matlab, and its exact commit; from `did_v1` to `did_v2`), and the run's report (every missing fact, by document and field, with the reason, plus the run's counts) is its data body. Built (did-schema part): `schemas/did_v2/migration.json` (a `method` block {term, parameters}, nothing else), its `persist` disposition, and `data_body.owner_id` may name a `migration`. Build pending: DID-matlab (write one `migration` document per run with the report as an `opaque_body`, and stop writing `base.needs_info`; how a missing required field is then accepted is proposed below and waits on the team), did-schema (remove `base.needs_info` and the field documentation that names it, once DID-matlab V2 no longer writes or reads it).

Proposed (not decided) [migration record details], under [migration record]; none of these is decided:
- (a) Validation. The schema keeps the fields required; while a migration runs, the validator accepts a missing required field only when that run's report lists it (Claude's option (c), "the migration validates against its report rather than against a field in the document", which Jess Haley's later questions built on but did not answer by itself). Re-validating migrated documents without the report fails on those fields. ANSWERED 2026-11-01: [gap accepted when the report lists it] (the report's layout is answered separately, [migration report is an attached file]).
- (b) The method term: EDAM:operation_0335 "Data formatting" (EDAM lists "Format conversion" as a synonym), registered in `did_v2_term_registry.json` as proposed. The parameter variables: IAO:0000010 "software" (DID-matlab) and IAO:0000129 "version number" (the commit) are already registered; no ontology term was found for "source format" or "target format" (OLS4 search, 2026-11-01), so those two are labels or new EMPTY terms, and a new EMPTY term needs the team's approval of its definition.
- (c) The report body: one `opaque_body`, `format` EDAM:format_3464 "JSON" (registered as proposed), its `owner_id` the `migration`. ANSWERED 2026-11-01: [migration report is an attached file] (the report is that attached JSON file; the term rows stay as proposed).
- (d) The `migration` document's `session_id`: the dataset entity when the run has one, else the session entity the run migrated. ANSWERED 2026-11-01: [migration record points at the dataset].
- (e) "One per run" in NDI: `ndi.migrate.local` calls `did2.convert.v1_to_v2` many times in one run, so the run's `migration` document is written once by `ndi.migrate.local` (NDI-matlab) from the reports of every call, and once by `did2.convert.fromV1Database` (DID-matlab) for a DID-only run. ANSWERED 2026-11-01: [migration record writer].
- (f) The decisions that say a gap is "named in needs_info" ([canonical absent only as needs info], [formulation amount needs info], [virus dilution needs info], [postnatal day 0 not stated], [acquisition time on its clock], [site frame is a coordinate system]) then read "listed in the migration report"; no meaning changes, only where the gap is written.

Decided by Jess Haley, 2026-11-01 [key covariate index]: ("2. Ok", to Claude's sketch answering docs/V2_PLAN.md GL8, in which each gene covariate of a mapping's `row` dimension reads `{variable: NCIT:C16612 "Gene", along: [0], key: 0, index: [5, 5, 9]}` and `{..., key: 1, index: [12, 30, 4]}`, and Claude said: "The new piece is `index`. Without it, a `key` covariate means what it does today: row k is entry k of the list. With it, row k is entry `index[k]` of the list.") a `key` covariate may carry `index`, one 0-based entry number per position of its dimension: position k is entry `index[k]` of the keyed document's value. Without `index`, position k is entry k, as before. For a gene-list mapping, the indices are copied from `mapping.tsv`'s gene index columns. Build pending: DID-matlab (`checkRules` and the builder accept `index` first), did-schema (`data.json`'s covariate `index` field and the rules naming it), DID-matlab (`gene_list_mapping.m`, still blocked on GL9).

Proposed (not decided) [gene list mapping terms], under [gene list mapping value shape], [gene list mapping method text] and [gene list mapping leftovers]; Claude's picks from the read-only term lookup of 2026-11-01, reported to Jess Haley ("Proceeding (existing terms, as you asked)") with no reply; none of these is decided:
- the `row` dimension's term: SIO:000752 "row" ("A row represents a single, implicitly structured data item in a table."), as proposed earlier in [covariate terms chosen]; [row term deferred] chooses it when a writer first produces sparse data, which this would be;
- the variable of the method parameter holding v1 `method`: NCIT:C150832 "Software Name" ("The literal identifier of the software program."). The template's own example, "GEF geneID/geneName pairing", is not a software name;
- the variable of the logical method parameter holding v1 `symmetric`: NCIT:C75919 "Symmetric Relationship" ("A binary relation such that if a is related to b then b is related to a.");
- the method term of an orthology mapping (docs/V2_PLAN.md GL10): a new EMPTY term "orthology inference" (Claude's recommendation; nothing found in OLS4 or Wikidata), or EDAM:operation_2945 "Data analysis", which [data analysis is the general method] makes the general method of a calculation, not of a relation.
The first three are in `schemas/registry/did_v2_term_registry.json` with "Proposed (not decided)" in their `decided` text.
